## [unreleased] — alpha

- feat(admin): **empty-state "Get started" launchers for the guided wizards, via one shared partial (#1999)** — the four `js/modules/admin-wizard.js` list-page wizards (#1992/#1993/#1995/#1996) were previously reachable only from a small header button, invisible to a curator who lands on a genuinely empty list. New `manage/includes/wizard-empty-state.php` (`ihymns_wizard_empty_state()`, modelled byte-for-byte on `slug-field.php`'s parameterised-partial idiom) renders an icon + heading + one-line body + a button that opens the SAME wizard modal the header trigger already opens (`data-bs-toggle="modal" data-bs-target="#<modalId>"` — Bootstrap binds every element sharing a target to one modal instance, so this is a second trigger, not a new mechanism). Wired into `external-link-types.php` (`!$types`, inside the existing schema-ready region), `songbooks.php` (the list table's empty `<tr>`, `$error`-aware, 11-col shape preserved), `venues.php` (`!$venues && $orgs` — deliberately gated on `$orgs`, not just `!$venues`, because a crafted `?org=<n>` on a zero-organisation install can otherwise leave `$selectedOrgId > 0` with an empty `$orgs`, which would point the launcher at a wizard modal that never rendered — rule #33's dead-launcher trap), and `organisations.php` (list-view-only, `headingTag: 'h3'` nested under the card's own `<h2>`). Editor2's two "pick a song" status strings gained a one-line mention of the existing Guided button. **Presentation only** — zero new server actions, zero new entitlement gates, zero new JS; `type="button"` on the launcher is load-bearing since the songbooks call site sits inside the reorder-save `<form>`. New `tests/php/test-wizard-empty-state.php` (88 assertions, mutation-proven, modelled on `test-service-setup-wizard.php`'s positional-gate technique): every call site's `modalId` literal is checked against a REAL `id="…"` on a `class="modal fade"` div plus a `data-bs-target="#…"` trigger in the SAME file (mutation-proven both directions — renaming the real modal id, and renaming the call site's argument, each independently go red); the partial's markup breadcrumb and function declaration each exist in exactly one file tree-wide; the external-link-types.php and venues.php call sites are proven positionally inside their pages' schema/`$orgs` gates; the partial's rendered output is checked (via direct in-process calls, and via a fresh-subprocess mutation proof for the parts that can't be re-`require`d in-process) for aria-hidden icons, non-empty visible button text, escaped user-facing text, and a load-bearing `modalId` format guard.
- feat(admin): **guided "New Song" wizard in Editor2 (#1997, epic #1992/#1983)** — the fifth wizard on the shared `js/modules/admin-wizard.js` stepper (#1992), and the first inside the editor itself rather than a `/manage/*` list page. A SEPARATE `#v2-new-wizard-btn` ("Guided") beside the toolbar's plain `#v2-new-btn` opens a new `#v2-new-wizard-modal` (`host:'bootstrap-modal'` — Editor2 already loads Bootstrap JS via `admin-footer.php` and drives four other modals the same way) walking a curator through: **Songbook** (the sidebar's real catalogue, `sidebar.getSongbooks()`, including zero-song books — #1679 A2 — with the hidden `#1783` staging book excluded via `load_index`'s own `pendingSongbook` key, never a hardcoded literal); **Number** (optional — Number is not a vanity field forced on every book, rule #44 — with a LIVE, debounced, display-only classification against `/api?action=missing_songs`'s authoritative read, one new client helper `missingSongNumbers()` in `api-client.js` mirroring `songKeyGet()`'s shape; the exported, mutation-tested `numberAvailability()` sorts a typed number into five outcomes — `free`/`beyond-max`/`hidden-held`/`taken`/`blank` — offering "Use next free" and, on a taken number, "Open it instead"; only `taken` blocks Next); **Title** + free-text alt-title chips; **Starting structure** (0-10 verses + optional Chorus/Bridge toggles, seeded in `structure-tab.js`'s own client shape, order Verse 1 → Chorus → Verse 2..N → Bridge); and **Review**. **Zero new server endpoints** (rule #22): `onFinish` mirrors Editor2's own Missing-Numbers `runPrefill()` step for step — a fresh (cache-bypassing) availability re-check to catch a same-session race, then `create_song` → `metadata_field_update` (field `number`) → `song_alt_title_add` × N → `components_replace` (mode `'replace'`, which is the ONE gate onto `lyricLinesWriteComponents()` — rule #25; the wizard never composes a SongId itself, nor writes a lyric line by any other path) — every step after `create_song` is non-fatal and precisely worded ("Created CP-x but couldn't set number 45 — set it on the Metadata tab"), and the song is ALWAYS opened afterwards, matching `runPrefill()`'s own "the editor is the recovery surface" posture. A Finish-time availability conflict routes back to the Number step (`wizard.goTo(1)` + a step alert) rather than minting a duplicate. **Purely additive**: the plain `#v2-new-btn`/`#v2-new-modal` handler, the Missing-Numbers `?songbook=&number=` prefill, and the `?duplicate=` flow are byte-identical, untouched. New `manage/editor/v2/new-song-wizard.js` (`mountNewSongWizard(ctx)`, mounted ONCE at boot beside `mountSidebar()` — never per-song like `mountTabs()`); wizard inputs carry `id="v2-nsw-*"` and **no** `name=` attributes (rule #43). New `tests/php/test-new-song-wizard.php` (69 assertions, mutation-proven, modelled on `test-songbook-wizard.php`): the manual button/modal/`runPrefill()` wiring stays intact (each marker anchored on its OWN bounded source region, the #1840-k lesson); the wizard is genuinely wired (markup + import + `mountNewSongWizard(`/`createWizard(` calls); no raw `fetch(` or bare api2 action-name string appears outside the injected `ctx.api.*` methods; `components_replace` still delegates to `ed2_persistComponents(`; Editor2's and api2.php's role gates agree (`hasRole(…, 'editor')`); and `tests/test-editor-deep-links.js` stays green (no new deep-link param). The five-outcome `numberAvailability()` truth table is proven FUNCTIONALLY (not by text match) in a new `tests/test-new-song-wizard-availability.js` — since the wizard module's own top-level browser import doesn't resolve under plain Node, this test extracts and `new Function()`-compiles the real, current function body straight from the tracked source and runs it against real inputs, with its own mutation proofs (a deliberately broken precedence copy correctly flips a hidden-held number to a false "taken").
- feat(admin): **guided "New Organisation + licence" wizard on `/manage/organisations` + a shared organisation-CREATE core across page/wizard/API, incl. its first admin API twin (#1996, epic #1992/#1983)** — the fourth wizard on the shared `js/modules/admin-wizard.js` stepper (#1992). Before this, organisation CREATE was inlined straight into the page's `create` POST case with **no admin API twin at all** — the only existing `organisation_create` API action is a DIFFERENT product (the CONSUMER self-service "create my own org" endpoint: any authenticated user, auto-suffix-dedups the slug, drops licence/active/city, inserts the caller as owner — untouched here). **The extraction**: new `includes/organisation_admin.php` — `orgAdminValidateCreate()`/`orgAdminCreate()` move the page's pre-#1996 `create` case verbatim (field parsing → the 7-column INSERT → its schema-tolerant `PhysicalCity`/`PhysicalCityId` UPDATE), with the INSERT now wrapped so a `uq_Slug` 1062 race closes the pre-check/INSERT TOCTOU as a 409 rather than a generic 500; `orgAdminApplyLicenceRows()` is a thin loop over the EXISTING `orgLicenceUpsert()` (`includes/org_licence_admin.php`, #1969 — never forked); `orgAdminMemberAdd()` is the 3-column member upsert, now the ONE write path for both the page's `add_member` case AND the new `admin_organisation_member_add` re-point (rule #22). **The #1986 finer gate, enforced twice over**: the licence STEP is omitted from the wizard's DOM when the caller lacks `manage_org_licences` (page-level `$canEditOrgLicences`, unchanged), but that omission is UX only — `orgAdminValidateCreate()` takes `$canEditOrgLicences` as an explicit parameter and FORCES the primary `licence_type`/`licence_number` to `none`/empty when false (the CREATE-time degenerate case of #1986's UPDATE-side "preserve, don't reject" — a new org has nothing to preserve), and BOTH JSON-speaking funnels (the wizard branch and the new API twin) independently resolve `userHasEntitlement('manage_org_licences', …)` and wrap the separate multi-licence-row step in its own `if ($canEditOrgLicences)` guard immediately before calling `orgAdminApplyLicenceRows()` — a caller with `manage_organisations` but not `manage_org_licences` cannot set licences via either path. **The wizard** (Organisation → Licences [only when `manage_org_licences`, repeatable rows, first row becomes the primary; degrades to a single type+number row with no add/remove when `tblOrganisationLicences` isn't migrated] → Members [optional, repeatable rows via the SAME `iHymnsPlaceSearch.attach()` + api2 `user_search` picker + resolved-pick guard groups.php's own Add-a-member picker uses] → Review → a DONE pane reporting each write's own outcome, mirroring #1995's shape) is **server-orchestrated in ONE new page JSON action**, `wizard_create_organisation` — unlike #1995's venues wizard, which client-orchestrates three PRE-EXISTING `api.php` actions, this capability was brand-new, so it lives behind one endpoint per rule #22's "extract first." That action sits BEFORE the classic forms' legacy `validateCsrf()` gate and carries its own `validateCsrfRequest()` check (the #1992 external-link-types shape, not #1993's songbooks shape — this page's whole POST handler was never already `validateCsrfRequest()`-gated). The wizard structurally reads no `org_id` from the request — the freshly-minted id is the only one it ever touches, so cross-tenant injection is impossible by construction, not by a runtime check. Member rows are individually verified server-side (role ∈ `ORG_MEMBER_ROLES`, user exists + `IsActive=1`) before the upsert; a licence/member row failure after the org itself committed is REPORTED per-row on the DONE pane, never rolled back. **The new API twin**, `admin_organisation_create` (beside `admin_organisation_update`), is the native-app/Swagger-console twin of both web funnels, delegating to the identical core, gated on the same `manage_organisations` entitlement (rule #1587) with no per-action `validateCsrfRequest()` — confirmed covered by the file's existing global `X-Requested-With` POST gate, same as its `admin_organisation_*` siblings; `api-docs.yaml` updated in the same change (`AdminOrganisationCreateInput` schema + path item). Wizard inputs carry `id="orgwiz-*"` and **no** `name=` attributes (rule #43) — collected into the fetch body by JS, with two repeatable-row field-name families (`licence_row_*[]`, `member_*[]`) deliberately DISTINCT from the primary `licence_type`/`licence_number` singular fields to avoid a genuine PHP `parse_str()` gotcha verified live: `foo=A&foo[]=B` silently clobbers the scalar into an array on the SAME `$_POST` key. New `tests/php/test-organisation-wizard.php` (122 assertions, mutation-proven, modelled on `test-songbook-wizard.php`): all three funnels delegate to the shared core with no case/branch-level `INSERT INTO tblOrganisations`; INSERT literals pinned tree-wide (`tblOrganisations` exactly 2 — the core + the untouched consumer case; `tblOrganisationLicences` exactly 2, both inside `org_licence_admin.php`; `tblOrganisationMembers` exactly 4, each traced to its expected home); the #1986 finer gate is proven wrapped around `orgAdminApplyLicenceRows(` in BOTH JSON funnels via a nested-brace-aware block extractor (mutation-proven by stripping the guard in a copy of each real body); CSRF-by-position; the wizard branch provably can't fall through to the page's HTML error catch; a structural IDOR proof (no `$_POST['org_id']` read anywhere in the branch, mutation-proven by injecting one); the manual form's field reads still match real `name=` controls (slug indirectly, via the `slug-field.php` #1870 partial); and wiring (trigger/modal/stepper import/`createWizard(`/the shared `user_search` picker). `test-manage-action-api-coverage.php` FIXED a pre-existing wrong mapping (`create` pointed at the consumer `organisation_create`, not the now-real admin twin) and gained the new `wizard_create_organisation` entry; `test-api-gate-parity.php` gained `admin_organisation_create`; `test-error-page-coverage.php` gained the new same-origin JSON 409 site; `tests/php/fixtures/orphan-allowlist.php` gained `admin_organisation_create` (API-first surface, same D1-default-A posture as its five `admin_organisation_*` siblings — no first-party in-repo caller, by design, matching `admin_songbook_create`'s own entry); one a11y fix (an icon-only member-row remove button needed `aria-label`). Follow-up (not this change, pre-existing and not inherited by the wizard): the manual "Add an organisation" form has never written a primary-licence join row — only the update-form sync does.
- feat(admin): **guided "Live Service setup" wizard on `/manage/venues` (#1995, epic #1992)** — the third wizard on the shared `js/modules/admin-wizard.js` stepper (#1992), and the first with a HYBRID shape (owner-confirmed D1): a Step 0 mode-chooser that teaches the Quick Live Follow (#1268, no setup — "Go Live" from any song) vs Service Mode (#1323, venue-wide join code) distinction curators keep confusing (rule #26), then, for Service Mode, a CONFIG-only flow — venue → service time → optional presentation-app driver key — that creates/updates rows and links out to the existing consoles (`/manage/service-projection`, `/manage/service-lead`) to actually go live. It never starts a session itself. **Zero new server endpoints**: `onFinish` orchestrates, client-side and sequentially, THREE EXISTING `api.php` actions the #1969/#1770 write cores already expose — `org_admin_venue_save` → `org_admin_schedule_save` (skipped on a "meet ad-hoc" opt-out) → `service_driver_key_mint` (skipped unless opted in, and non-fatal on failure — the venue/schedule stand either way, with the DONE pane pointing at the Projector Screen to mint later). Transport is a bare `fetch()` + `X-Requested-With` + `credentials:'same-origin'` (the `/manage` house pattern; `js/utils/api-client.js` is PWA-only, rule #31), never a page-local AJAX case. `state.venueId`/`state.scheduleId` are held from each response so a retry after a later-step failure re-saves (UPDATEs) rather than re-creates the venue — there is no name-uniqueness constraint to lean on instead. A save failure routes the wizard back to the offending step with the server's own error text (`goTo()` + the step's `[data-wiz-alert]`); the minted driver key is shown exactly once in a DONE pane, never persisted client-side. The manual venue/schedule create/edit/delete forms + their POST handlers on `manage/venues.php`, and the driver-key card on `manage/service-projection.php`, are untouched (byte-identical) — this is purely additive. Wizard inputs carry `id="svcwiz-*"` and **no** `name=` attributes (rule #43), collected into the fetch bodies by JS. Reuses the shared location typeahead (`window.iHymnsPlaceSearch.attach()`), the org list, `$TZ_LIST`, `IHYMNS_VENUE_RECURRENCE_KINDS` and `SERVICE_DRIVER_KEY_PROTOCOLS` — never a page-local copy of any of those vocabularies (rule #35). Gated on the page's own `manage_organisations` entitlement (unchanged) — the API twins' broader org-admin gate (`userCanActOnOrg`) is a superset, so page∩API stays the (stricter) page gate. New `tests/php/test-service-setup-wizard.php` (133 assertions, mutation-proven, modelled on `test-external-link-wizard.php`/`test-songbook-wizard.php`): the manual dispatch surface (`venue_save`/`venue_delete`/`schedule_save`/`schedule_delete`) still calls its shared `venueAdmin*()` core with no raw SQL of its own and its forms still feed every field the core reads; exactly one `INSERT INTO tblOrgVenues`/`tblOrgServiceSchedules`/`tblServiceDriverKeys` exists tree-wide (all three inside their shared core, never a fork) and the wizard's own isolated block calls exactly the three expected `api.php` actions and no SQL; both `org_admin_venue_save`'s and `org_admin_schedule_save`'s existing-row cross-tenant IDOR re-checks (the 2026-08-29 F1 fix) are still present and correctly ordered before their write call (also confirms `test-security-schedule-idor.php` stays green); gate parity + schema-ready placement; the shared `createWizard()` stepper singleton stays intact (`test-external-link-wizard.php` still green); the wizard block carries neither a `service_session_start` nor a `live_follow_create` literal (this is a CONFIG wizard, never a RUNTIME one); and the minted key is never written to `localStorage`/`sessionStorage`.
- feat(admin): **guided "New Songbook" wizard on `/manage/songbooks` + a shared songbook-CREATE core across page/wizard/API (#1993, epic #1992/#1983)** — the second wizard on the shared `js/modules/admin-wizard.js` stepper (#1992), and the first time all three create funnels (the page's manual "Add a songbook" form, the new wizard, and the `admin_songbook_create` API action) share ONE validate+write core instead of two drifted inline copies (rule #22). **The extraction**: `includes/songbook_admin.php`'s `songbookAdminValidateCreate()`/`songbookAdminCreate()` moved the page's pre-#1993 `create` case verbatim (field-parsing → the 23-bind INSERT → its four schema-tolerant secondary UPDATEs → the #1865 publisher seed → the #1909 webhook emit), with the INSERT now wrapped so a `uq_Abbreviation` 1062 race closes the pre-check/INSERT TOCTOU as a 409 rather than a generic 500. **API parity fix**: `admin_songbook_create` gained `display_abbr` (#1332), `publication_city`/`publication_city_id`, `is_public_domain` + the OpenLibrary id pair (#1765 Features 2/3) and the #1865 publisher-registry seed it never had, while dropping the dead `internet_archive_url` input the web form removed in #1765 Feature 7 (still valid on `admin_songbook_update`, which has its own untouched field-parsing block — `api-docs.yaml`'s `SongbookWriteInput` updated in lockstep, per-field CREATE/UPDATE scope annotated). **The wizard** (Identity → Code → Details → Review): the Code step is the highest-value screen (rule #27) — live client-side check against the same charset/length/IL-reservation grammar `validateSongbookAbbr()` enforces server-side, plus a same-page seeded-abbreviation uniqueness check (never authoritative — the server's pre-check + the INSERT's own race guard still decide); a non-blocking warning (not a hard stop) when the code contains a digit, since a digit code silently degrades the router/favorites/related-songs/OG-image consumers that assume letters-only. Details (Publisher via the existing `iHymnsPlaceSearch.attach()` picker, publication year, copyright, affiliation, language via the shared `ietf-language-picker.php` partial) is a lean, all-optional subset per rule #44 — Colour/DisplayOrder/city/public-domain/13 authority ids/series/parent/compilers/alt-names/external-links stay edit-only. MARCXML import (page case + `admin_songbook_marcxml_import`) deliberately stays OUT of the shared core — its 4-column stub INSERT differs materially from the 23-bind create shape; both sites are documented and enumerated by the new guard. New `wizard_create_songbook` case (an ordinary case in the page's existing `switch`, already behind its page-wide `validateCsrfRequest()` + `manage_songbooks` gate — simpler than #1992's ELT wizard) returns `{error, field}` on failure so the client routes 409/400 back to the right step by a structured key, never by matching error prose (rule #35). Wizard inputs carry `id="sbwiz-*"` and **no** `name=` attributes — collected into the POST body by JS — so the pre-existing `test-songbook-form-parity.php` Add/Edit guard stays green untouched. New `tests/php/test-songbook-wizard.php` (136 assertions, mutation-proven): all three funnels delegate to the shared core with no case-body-level `INSERT INTO tblSongbooks`; the INSERT literal is pinned to its five honest tree-wide sites (the core, the two MARCXML cases, and two pre-existing unrelated stub-creators — `song_importers.php`'s bulk-import auto-create and `editor/api2.php`'s "Pending duplicates" fixture seeder — discovered, not assumed, while writing the guard); gate parity; CSRF-by-position; the wizard case provably can't fall through to the page's HTML error catch; the manual form's field reads (now one level into the shared core) still match real `name=` controls or the documented partial require-chain; and a real functional truth table against `validateSongbookAbbr()` (`''`/`MP`/11-char/`MP-1`/`mp 1`/`il`/`ILS`/`ILSONGS`/`IL9`). `test-work-registry-pickers.php` §F re-pointed one level through the same delegation (was asserting the pre-extraction inline shape); `test-manage-action-api-coverage.php` and `test-error-page-coverage.php` (a new same-origin JSON 409) extended. Follow-up: folding the two admin-facing MARCXML funnels into the shared core, tracked for a future pass rather than done opportunistically here.
- fix(a11y): **table header `scope` + decorative-icon `aria-hidden` across admin (#1990)** — deferred mechanical follow-up to the WCAG 2.1 AA pass (e92e9e38). M2: `scope="col"` on 308 admin-table column headers (35 manage pages + `revisions-tab.js`'s JS-built scalar table) + `scope="row"` on the 5 genuine vertical row headers (`intapps-status.php`); insert-only-when-absent (42 already-scoped cells untouched). M8: `aria-hidden="true"` on ~649 PHP + 45 JS decorative Bootstrap icons that sit beside visible text; `role="img"` + `aria-label` on the 2 meaningful non-interactive glyphs (musicians "has notes" sticky, venues map-pin badge); `aria-label` on 11 icon-only JS buttons named only via `title`. The bare icon-only-control class was already fixed by e92e9e38's M4 (zero remained), so the blind pass could not create a nameless control. Guard `tests/php/test-a11y-static-checks.php` extended with `a11yThMissingScope()` + `a11yIconAccessibility()` (PHP-block-neutralised, tag-local, mutation-proven, narrow enough not to flag decorative-beside-text or `title`-named controls per rule #34). 68 files, idempotent tag-local edits, no logic touched. `iconBtn()` triplication flagged as follow-up #1991.
- fix(security): **retire the webhook reveal-existing-secret path (#1987)** — the API was already show-once (only mint/rotate emit a secret) and API keys are hash-at-rest; the one remaining reveal-existing leak was the web page `manage/webhooks.php::reveal_secret` → `webhookSubscriptionRevealSecret()`, which #1909 had deliberately allowed. Retired: the dispatcher case, the button, and the core function; the `$revealSecret` one-shot display (create/rotate) and the signing decrypt seam `webhookSecretReveal()` (delivery + verification still need it) are untouched. "Hash-at-rest" (the issue's original title) is impossible for a signing secret — the server needs plaintext to compute each outgoing HMAC — so it is encrypted-at-rest, which was already correct. Docs (help/api-docs/wiki) + design-doc addendum updated. Guard `tests/php/test-webhook-secret-show-once.php` (33 assertions, tree-derived, mutation-proven: every `webhookSecretReveal()` call site must sit in the signing/verification allow-set and never route the plaintext to a response). Follow-up #1989 filed to re-wrap pre-cutover plaintext table-held secrets (the #1909 A.14 card never filed).
- feat(api): **Works "extras" over the API — scalars, origin city, membership, external links (#1988, epic #1983)** — closed the API-coverage gap `admin_work_*` batch 4a explicitly deferred. Seven closures/inline blocks extracted verbatim from `manage/works.php` into `includes/work_admin.php` (`workExtraColumnsPresent`/`workParseExtraFields`/`workCheckExtraUniqueness`/`workPersistExtraFields`/`workPersistOriginCity`/`workSongsReplace`/`workParentCycleSafe` — the last consolidating two byte-identical cycle-walk copies), page re-pointed to one-line delegates (rule #22). `admin_work_create`/`_update` gained 12 extras+origin_city keys (create defaults absent keys; update uses key-present-preserve per rule #45); new `admin_work_members_replace` (tblWorkSongs, pre-validates song ids) + `admin_work_external_links_replace` (via shared `saveExternalLinksForRow()`, table-probed 503). All gate `manage_works` (rule #1587); CSRF via the global `X-Requested-With` gate; api-docs in lockstep (rule #48). `SongData::getWork()` gained round-trip keys (`members[].sortOrder`, `links[].typeId`, `musicBrainzWorkMbid`/`copyrightHolderId`/`originCity`/`originCityId`) so a replace can be seeded from the read (rule #33). Guard `tests/php/test-api-coverage-work-extras.php` (60 assertions, mutation-proven).
- fix(api): **entitlement-gate parity + licence-field authz gap for admin write actions (#1986)** — 11 admin write endpoints in `api.php` guarded on a bare `in_array($authUser['Role'], ['admin','global_admin'])` swapped to the same `userHasEntitlement()` gate their sibling `manage/*.php` page enforces (rule #1587; behaviour-neutral today — each key defaults to exactly `['admin','global_admin']`, parsed from the live `ENTITLEMENTS` array to prove it). Separately closed a real field-level gap the sweep surfaced: `admin_organisation_update` wrote `LicenceType`/`LicenceNumber` and ran `orgLicenceSyncSet()` unconditionally, while the page gates those behind the finer `manage_org_licences` (preserve-not-reject) — the API now mirrors the page (resolve `$canEditOrgLicences`, preserve the stored licence fields when false, skip the sync). Guard `tests/php/test-api-gate-parity.php` extended (20 gated actions, mutation-proven the licence preserve).
- feat(a11y): **opt-in "Emphasise Links" mode (#1984)** — resolves the deferred S1 (prose-link contrast) not by reversing rule #18's understated default but with an opt-in per-device toggle (Settings → Appearance) mirroring the CVD-mode mechanism exactly: `localStorage.ihymns_link_emphasis` → `data-ihymns-linkcue="on"` on `<html>` at the same call sites CVD uses (`settings.js` `applyTheme()` public / `admin-theme-init.php` pre-CSS admin), two zero-specificity `:where([data-ihymns-linkcue="on"]) a / .song-meta-link` rules on a new `--link-emphasis-color` token (dark theme uses an explicit `#6366f1` because the dark `--accent-solid` `#818cf8` measures only 2.72:1; light 3.28:1 / dark 4.08:1, WCAG-verified). No underline/border; Bootstrap chrome untouched. Guard `tests/php/test-link-emphasis-mode.php` (33 assertions, mutation-tested). CLAUDE.md rule #18 updated.
- feat(pp7): **ProPresenter 7 chord round-trip — import ↔ export (#1968 P6, folds into #1080)** — reference-validated correction to the epic's working premise: PP7 does NOT store chords as inline `[G]` brackets in the slide RTF (that is only ProPresenter's editing metaphor). Chords are positioned protobuf `CustomAttribute{IntRange range, string chord}` rows inside `element.text.attributes.custom_attributes[]` over CLEAN plain text — confirmed against the vendored `graphicsData.proto`, greyshirtguy/Pro7ChordEditorWin and xilefmusics/chordlib (design: `.claude/propresenter-chords-plan.md`). Decoder (`includes/propresenter7_decode.php`): `pp7DecodeCustomAttribute()`/`pp7DecodeIntRange()` decode the chord oneof (field 7); import (`includes/song_importers.php`) buckets chords to per-line positioned string cells (UTF-16→code-point, `PP7_CHORD_NEWLINE_UNITS` isolating the plan's one open newline-weight question for a real chord file to settle) and rides the EXISTING `chords` parallel array through `lyricLinesWriteComponents()` (rule #25 — no new SQL); export (`manage/editor/propresenter-export.js`) emits `custom_attributes[]` at displayed columns with `buildRTF()` UNCHANGED (a chord symbol NEVER enters `rtf_data`), no `chord_pro` write in v1. Correctness fix caught by the fixture: proto3 omits a scalar at its default, so a chord genuinely at UTF-16 offset 0 encodes with `start` absent — decode now treats absence as 0, not malformed. Also fixed the chords-textarea `l.trim()` that destroyed leading positional padding in BOTH `editor.js` and the v2 `structure-tab.js`. Guards (all mutation-proven, rule #34): `tests/php/test-pp7-chord-import.php`, `tests/php/test-pp7-decode.php`, chord rows in `test-pp7-roundtrip.php` (two-independent-halves closure), `test-propresenter-export.js` (incl. the premise-guard: no `[` ever in `rtf_data`), `test-propresenter-static-csp.js`, `tests/test-editor-chord-trim.js`. Non-circular fixture `tools/pp7-gen-chord-fixture.js` builds the chord `.pro` via protobufjs reflection (never our own exporter), marked reference-derived pending owner checklist D4 (a real chord-bearing file).
- feat(propresenter): **ProPresenter auto-advance timeline capture — DORMANT groundwork, OFF by default (#1968 P6)** — owner steer: build the schema/capture now, flesh out playback later. `Presentation.timeline` (field 17) was previously presence-only ("P6 fodder"); `includes/propresenter7_decode.php` now fully decodes it — `pp7DecodeTimeline()`/`pp7DecodeTimelineCue()` read `Timeline.cues` (field 1, the clean slide-advance schedule: `trigger_time`/`cue_id`/`name`) and unpack `trigger_time`'s wire-type-1 double little-endian. **Field-semantics correction made during implementation:** `Timeline.cues_v2` (field 11) is deliberately NEVER consulted — independently decoding two real multi-cue ProPresenter exports proved it is a SUPERSET carrying `ACTION_TYPE_CLEAR_GROUP`/`ACTION_TYPE_CLEAR` automation entries (not the auto-advance schedule); preferring it, as an earlier draft of this spec called for, would have captured automation actions as if they were slide triggers. New dormant table `tblSongPresentationCues` (`appWeb/.sql/migrate-pp7-timeline-groundwork.php` + `schema.sql` mirror, rule #19/#20 — VARCHAR `Source` vocab, `ArrangementName` multiplicity discriminator, `ComponentId` reserved NULL for later mapping work) + the `pp7_timeline_import_enabled` app-setting toggle (seeded `'0'`). Capture helpers `includes/pp7_timeline.php` (`pp7TimelineImportEnabled()`/`pp7TimelineTableExists()`/`pp7TimelineStore()`, delete-then-insert, STRICT-mysqli safe) are wired into `_bulkImport_processPro7()` — the ONE shared single-file import pipeline every `.pro`/`.probundle`/`.proplaylist` path funnels through — behind the toggle and its own try/catch (non-blocking). Verified no-op at the toggle's shipped default. Explicitly OUT of scope: no playback, no auto-advance UI — that is later work. Guard: `tests/php/test-pp7-timeline.php` (mutation-proven — 5 mutations, each confirmed RED then reverted, including the cues_v2 false-positive regression and a DB-optional behavioural block mirroring `test-song-media-visibility.php`'s "the mysqli type hint blocks a fake" idiom). Synthetic fixtures + generator: `tests/fixtures/propresenter/timeline/`, `tools/pp7-gen-timeline-fixture.js`. 218 PHP / 81 node.
- feat(org): **Multiple licences per organisation — global-admin editor + consolidation + two data-integrity fixes (#1969)** — a church routinely holds several licences at once (CCLI for the lyrics, MRL for the music, an iHymns plan). The storage (`tblOrganisationLicences`, one row per type, with number/expiry/active/notes) and the member self-service editor already existed; the gaps were the **global-admin** surface and three latent bugs the audit surfaced. **New shared core** `includes/org_licence_admin.php` (rule #22) — validate/list/upsert/update/delete + a **non-destructive** set-reconcile — now backs all four surfaces (the two `/manage/*` pages and both native API actions), replacing four drifted copies. **`/manage/organisations`** (global admin) gains a per-row "Licence details" editor (number/expiry/active/notes per held licence) to match `/manage/my-organisations`. **Bugs fixed:** (1) the global-admin form + the `admin_organisation_update` API both **wiped every licence's metadata on every save** (a DELETE-all + re-INSERT) — now a metadata-preserving reconcile; (2) two API paths **hard-coded a licence-key list that was missing `mrl` and `custom`** (rule #9) — so the API literally could not record an MRL licence — now registry-validated; (3) the tier resolver (`ccli_validator.php`) **ignored a licence's `ExpiresAt`** while the CCLI gate honoured it, so an expiry set in the UI was only half-enforced — now both filter it. No schema change (every column already existed). Guard: `tests/php/test-org-licence-admin.php` (tree-derived, mutation-proven — bans a re-added key list, a whole-org wipe, or a dropped expiry predicate). 217 PHP / 81 node.
- feat(auth): **Signed-in devices — web auto-naming + a rename affordance (#1975)** — the Settings → Signed-in devices list showed every device as "Unnamed device" because nothing populated the #1409 metadata columns, and there was no way to name one. **Auto-name:** the server now derives a friendly label ("Chrome on Windows", "Safari on iPhone") from the request `User-Agent` at token-issue time — a PURE `apiTokenBrowserLabelFromUA()` + `apiTokenWebDeviceFallback()` applied inside the ONE function every web sign-in funnels through (`apiTokenDeviceMetaStore`), so `auth_login`/`auth_apple`/email-login/registration all auto-name with **no client change and no schema column**. It fills `platform='web'` + a name ONLY when the client sent no platform AND the UA is a recognised browser; an explicit platform (a native app) is always respected, and an unrecognised UA is left untouched so a native/other client is never mislabelled a browser. **Rename:** a new `device_rename` endpoint (own-only `UserId = ?` in the UPDATE, `validateCsrfRequest()` same-origin gate, per-user rate limit, 409 on an un-migrated install, empty name clears back to the auto label) + an inline per-row rename affordance in `devices.js` (pencil → field → Save/Cancel, Enter/Escape, focus management, available on "This device" too). The design chose **server-side UA derivation over editing ~6 client sign-in bodies** (which would half-ship the moment one was missed — rule #33/#35); native apps still send their own platform/name (unchanged). Guards (tree-derived, mutation-proven): `tests/php/test-api-token-device-meta.php` (UA-parser + fallback + cleaner truth tables, a **token-mint coverage check** — every `INSERT INTO tblApiTokens` either stores metadata or carries a `no-device-meta:` reason marker — and `device_rename` hardening assertions) + `tests/test-batch8-ui-helpers.js` (rename UI helpers). `api-docs.yaml` documents `device_rename`. 216 PHP / 81 node.
- feat(export): **single-song `.probundle` media export — embed a public background video/image into the ProPresenter export (epic #1968, #1979)** — the single-song ProPresenter 7 export now produces a `.probundle` that EMBEDS the song's public background media (the P4 follow-on that was deferred at PR-4 ship time). When a song has a public `video` or `image` in `tblSongMedia` (`pickBackgroundMedia()` — first video, else first image; audio/pdf/sheet-music are never backgrounds; only PUBLIC rows reach here because the public `song_data` read is visibility-filtered, #1968 P4), `exportSongAsBundle()` fetches the media bytes from its same-origin `streamUrl`, writes them at the ZIP ROOT under a flat filename, and references them from the `.pro` via a **"Lyrics Background" media cue** (`ACTION_TYPE_MEDIA`). The media URL uses `ROOT_CURRENT_RESOURCE` with a **bare filename** on BOTH `element.url` and the type-specific mirror (`element.video.file.local_url` / `element.image.file.local_url`) — the portable "resolve next to this document" form. The background is a **palette cue group, NOT a step in the arrangement** (matching a real PP layout). No embeddable media — or a media fetch failure — **degrades cleanly to a bare `.pro`** (a lyric export must never fail on a media hiccup). No encoder regeneration was needed (the CSP-safe static encoder already carried the Media/URL/ACTION_TYPE_MEDIA types from PR-3). **Non-circular validation (the owner's #1 rule):** the new guard `tests/test-pp7-media-export.js` DECODES the media action inside the genuine `bussnet-testbild.probundle` (a real bundle exported FROM ProPresenter with its media inside) and asserts the exporter's emitted action matches that portable shape field-for-field — the anchor is a real file, not a self-consistent round-trip. TestBild is the right anchor: the owner's real video files reference media left IN PLACE on the authoring machine (machine-absolute `ROOT_USER_HOME`/`ROOT_USER_DOWNLOADS` + full `file://`), whereas an embedded-at-root export must use `ROOT_CURRENT_RESOURCE`, which is the only one of the three real roots that resolves on any machine — exactly why the deliberately-portable TestBild uses it. Guard is tree-derived + mutation-proven (5 mutations — swapped root, dropped mirror, wrong action type, bg-in-arrangement, image-over-video — each verified red). Remaining: a real-ProPresenter *open* of a media-bearing export stays an owner-checklist item (D4, non-blocking; it was always the export's final confirmation and is not circular because the wire shape is anchored to a real file). 81 node, all mutation-proven. Issue #1979.
- feat(media): **ProPresenter bundle/playlist media → `tblSongMedia`, admin-only until published (epic #1968, Phase 4)** — media referenced by an imported `.probundle`/`.proplaylist` (background videos, images) is now INGESTED into `tblSongMedia` and linked to the song, instead of the P2/P3 deferred-warning drop. Owner decision **D1**: imported media lands **curator-only** and is served publicly only when a curator publishes it. A new per-row publish state `tblSongMedia.Visibility` (`public | admin`, VARCHAR-not-ENUM, rule #20) drives an **always-active serving gate** — the ONE `includes/song_media_visibility.php` gates both the list emit (a SQL filter on every public `FROM tblSongMedia` read: `SongData::_songMediaMap()` — which closes 5 emit points at once incl. the shared-cache `page=song` fragment and `bulk_audio` — `getSongDetailExtras()`, and `songMediaRecomputeFlags()`) and the bytes (`song-media.php` answers **404-no-body** for an admin row to a non-curator, resolving the viewer from the `ihymns_auth` cookie so a signed-in curator's preview links keep working). It is a **verified no-op** for all existing content (every current row is `public`) and on un-migrated installs, deliberately NOT behind `content_gating_enabled` (an editorial publish state, not a tier cap), and **fail-CLOSED on the serve axis** for any unknown value. Ingest is **fail-closed + entirely dormant** behind `tblAppSettings.pp7_media_ingest_enabled` (default `'0'`) — a mechanism (not memory) against the shared-DB cross-channel leak, flipped by the owner only after this reaches `main`; a row that cannot be stored `admin` is never stored at all. The resolver (`_bulkImport_pp7ResolveMediaRef()`) matches a decoder media ref to a bundle entry by url-decoded basename + longest-suffix disambiguation, and **never guesses** — a tie or a miss is warned + skipped (attaching the wrong media is the false-positive class the epic bans); validation reuses the existing `SongMediaStorage::validateUpload()` (no second path, rule #42) and dedupes by SHA-256. `SongMediaStorage` gains `video`/`image` kinds (Kind is VARCHAR since #1090 — no DDL). The **editor2 Media tab** badges "Admin only" rows + a per-row Publish/Unpublish toggle + a "Publish all N" convenience (new `media_set_visibility` endpoint; no new entitlement); the public song page renders published `video` (`<video>`, Range-seekable) and `image` blocks (rule #33 — the publish is honoured), with a dormant one-line `CanPlayVideo` tier cap (rule #28, all inert while gating is off). Also fixes **#1977**: `PP7_ZIP_MAX_INPUT_BYTES` 25→100 MiB (was below the `.probundle` upload cap, silently rejecting a 25–100 MiB bundle post-upload). Fixtures are **lyric-sanitised derivatives** of the owner's genuine v21.4/v18.4 samples (decision D3), produced by the committed `tools/pp7-sanitise-fixture.js` (RTF dialect header + arrangements + CCLI + media refs preserved byte-for-byte; visible lyrics → "Sanitised line N", zero lyric leak verified). Guards (tree-derived, mutation-proven, rule #34): `test-song-media-visibility.php` (surface net + writer contract + PHP↔JS kind lockstep + the pure serve-decision truth table) and `test-pp7-media-ingest.php` (resolver/MIME/basename/sparse-exposure truth tables); en route, fixed a latent a11y-guard flaw (an interpolated PHP close-tag truncated its `<img>` match) and a `?>`-in-`//`-comment that had been about to make that whole test pass vacuously. The set-list/song → `.probundle` media **export** (CURRENT_RESOURCE URLs) is tracked as a follow-on (#1979) — deferred deliberately because validating a media-bearing export needs a real ProPresenter to open it, and shipping an un-triggered wire-shape helper would be the circular same-schema round-trip the owner's #1 rule bans. 215 PHP / 80 node, all mutation-proven. Issue #1976.
- feat(setlist): **Export an iHymns set list → ProPresenter `.proplaylist` (epic #1968, Phase 3, export direction)** — completes the `.proplaylist` round-trip. `exportSetlistAsProplaylist()` builds a `rv.data.PlaylistDocument` (root "PLAYLIST" → one playlist named after the set list → presentation items for song slots + header items for non-song slots, each presentation referencing the same arrangement UUID its exported `.pro` carries) and wraps it as the bundle's `data` entry alongside each song's `.pro` at the root — the same portable layout as the `.probundle` fix. A dedicated "Export .proplaylist" button lands in the set-list detail view. This required regenerating the CSP-safe **static protobuf encoder** to add `PlaylistDocument`; that regen was proven **byte-identical** for the existing `.pro`/`.probundle` export path (old vs new static module encode the same `Presentation` to the same SHA-256). Round-trip is validated JS-encoder → real PHP decoder (34/34, mutation-proven). 213 PHP / 80 node.
- feat(editor): **ProPresenter `.proplaylist` import → iHymns set list (epic #1968, Phase 3, import direction)** — a ProPresenter service order (`.proplaylist`) can now be imported: `_bulkImport_processProplaylist()` decodes the playlist's protobuf `data` entry (new `includes/propresenter7_playlist.php`, reusing the P1 wire-walker + P2 ZIP64 reader) and builds one iHymns set list — presentation items become songs (resolved to the bundled inner `.pro` by url-decoded basename and imported via the Phase-1 path, de-duped; or matched to an existing live song by normalised title when referenced-not-embedded), header/placeholder items become set-list slots, and `cue`/`planning_center`/unknown items are skipped with a warning. Nested playlists flatten in order. The set-list write reuses the app's own `SongsJson`/`SlotsJson` sanitisers + column-gating (no new authoritative write path); the owner is always the authenticated session user. Import is editor-gated (curator-first). Media inside a playlist bundle surfaces as a warning (ingest is Phase 4). Validated against 3 real MIT `.proplaylist` fixtures with the decoder cross-checked against protobufjs (independent decoder); all mutation-proven; 212 PHP / 80 node. The set list → `.proplaylist` **export** direction follows in a later PR.
- feat(editor): **ProPresenter `.probundle` import + genuine export bundle layout (epic #1968, Phase 2)** — a ProPresenter bundle (one or more presentations packaged with their media) can now be imported: `_bulkImport_processProbundle()` (`includes/song_importers.php`) extracts every inner `.pro` and runs each through the Phase-1 importer, so a multi-presentation bundle becomes multiple songs. The bundle reader is a NEW tolerant ZIP64 scanner (`includes/propresenter7_zip.php`) — genuine PP7-exported `.probundle` files are ZIP64 with an inconsistent end-of-central-directory that PHP `ZipArchive`, `unzip` and Python `zipfile` **all reject** (verified: `ZipArchive::open()` returns code 21 on the owner's real 2 MB bundle), so the reader never reads the central directory: it scans `PK\x03\x04` local file headers directly and resolves the `0xFFFFFFFF` size sentinels from the ZIP64 extra field. Media inside a bundle is surfaced in the import summary as a warning (filenames + count) rather than silently dropped — media **ingest** into `tblSongMedia` is Phase 4. Also fixes the **export** side: `exportAllAsBundle()` used to wrap every `.pro` under an invented `Documents/` folder plus a `manifest.json`; genuine bundles place the `.pro` at the ROOT with no manifest (the inner `.pro` is the manifest), so the old layout is corrected. Anti-false-positive harness continues: cross-validated against the two real MIT bundle fixtures **and** a synthesised `synthetic-zip64.probundle` that reproduces the exact 98-byte central-directory overstatement genuine PP writes — its defining test asserts `ZipArchive` rejects it while the hand-reader succeeds, so CI durably guards the real breaking path. Known limitation: the v2 import page (`api2.php`) keeps its blanket 25 MiB single-file cap, while the editor's dedicated `bulk_import_probundle` endpoint accepts up to 100 MiB (follow-up). All mutation-proven; PHP 210/210, node 80/80.
- feat(editor): **ProPresenter 7+ `.pro` import — lyrics, structure, arrangements (#885, epic #1968)** — a genuine ProPresenter 7+ (through 21.4-verified) `.pro` file can now be imported straight into iHymns as a new song, alongside the existing ChordPro/OpenLyrics/Pro6/EasyWorship/FreeShow/PPTX importers. Decoding is a NEW, hand-rolled server-side proto3 wire-walker (`includes/propresenter7_decode.php`) — not a browser decoder, since the enforcing nonce CSP already forces the *export* side onto an eval-free static protobuf module (#1788), and a server-side walker also naturally hosts the later `.probundle`/`.proplaylist` phases. `_bulkImport_parsePro7()`/`_bulkImport_processPro7()` (`includes/song_importers.php`) turn the decoded presentation into the same neutral song shape every other importer produces: cue groups fold to iHymns section types via a widened `_bulkImport_pro7GroupType()` (the 16 seeded `tblSongPartTypes` slugs, plus rule-#45 display labels for anything that doesn't fold cleanly), `selected_arrangement`/`arrangements[]` resolve into `tblSongs.ArrangementJson`, and the `ccli` block supplies title/writers/copyright/CCLI number. The shared `_bulkImport_rtfToText()` RTF extractor gained dual-dialect support (Apple "Cocoa RTF" soft-return line breaks from macOS PP, `\rtf0`/`\par`/`\csgenericrgb` from Windows PP 7.13+), cp1252-aware `\'xx` decoding, and surrogate-pair `\uN?` handling — strict correctness fixes that also benefit the existing Pro6/EasyWorship/Proclaim imports, plus a dominant-font lyric-selection rule that drops a real PP7-Mac RTF-writer artifact (a small sub-dominant text run glued onto the front of the real lyric on some genuine exports) without touching legitimate multi-run slides. `.pro` was genuinely ambiguous with ChordPro's own `.pro` convention, so routing is now a content-sniff (binary/protobuf → PP7, `<RVPresentationDocument>`/`<?xml` → Pro6, else ChordPro) on both the client (`editor.js`, convenience only) and server (`api2.php`'s `import_file`, the ZIP importer's per-entry router — the authoritative check, via one shared `_bulkImport_sniffProDialect()`). Media, themes, and the timeline are later phases of epic #1968 (not built here); translation-layer text elements (more than one text box per slide) are counted and flagged in the import summary rather than silently dropped, with `tblLyricLineTranslations` noted as their eventual home. **Anti-false-positive harness** (the owner's rule for this epic — no decoder/exporter change ships validated only against its own output): `includes/propresenter7_decode.php` is cross-validated field-for-field against an INDEPENDENT decoder (protobufjs reflection) on 9 real, MIT-licensed, third-party `.pro` fixtures (chrismbarr/bussnet, `tests/fixtures/propresenter/`, committed with attribution); the full parse walk is validated against hand-reviewed expected output for those same real files; a new PHP↔JS lockstep guard (`tests/test-pp7-label-roundtrip.js`) proves every section name/letter the exporter's `COMPONENT_LABEL_MAP` can emit folds back to its original type through the importer's map; and a new export→import closure test (`tests/php/test-pp7-roundtrip.php`) builds a small song, exports it through the REAL exporter, re-imports it through the REAL importer, and asserts it comes back unchanged — two independently-implemented halves of this app (browser-JS encoder, PHP decoder) agreeing on the same bytes, not a same-process self-decode. All guards are mutation-proven (rule #34). PHP 208/208, node 80/80.

- fix(pwa): **offline downloads survive every deploy; refresh only on real content change (#1962)** — a version bump must never wipe a user's downloaded media, and downloaded content must refresh only when that specific backend file changed. Two gaps broke this: the newer `tblSongMedia → /song-media/<id>` family (#853) — the **only** route for MusicXML, plus curator-uploaded audio/PDF/MIDI — was not recognised by the service worker at all (its `<a download>` responses landed in the version-keyed shell bucket and were **wiped on every deploy**; `<audio>` Range/206 requests were cached nowhere), and all `MEDIA_CACHE` content was pure cache-first, never revalidated. Fixed with one mechanism and no migration (`Sha256` already exists in `tblSongMedia`): the SW now matches `/song-media/` as media ahead of the navigation branch, the public streamUrl is content-stamped `?song=<SongId>&v=<sha256:12>` (a changed file = a new key fetched once, riding the existing #131 update flow and the `autoUpdateOfflineSongs` choice), and the media handler gains stale-while-revalidate conditional GETs where a 304/error/offline never mutates the cache. `bulk_audio` now includes uploaded audio and syncs conditionally, `audio-media.php` gains validators, downloaded songbook fragments re-warm at download+activate, and `songs_index` moves to a version-independent bucket. Guards (rule #34, mutation-proven): new `tests/test-offline-media-coverage.js` + extended `tests/test-offline-cache-policy.js`. PR #1964.

- feat(ci): **conventional-commit semver auto-versioning with dynamic tag minting on alpha (#1963)** — alpha now visibly increments and tags are minted dynamically by CI (extending the dormant #1899 scheme): `feat`→minor, `feat!`/`BREAKING CHANGE`→major, everything else→build-only (a safe structural default — a mislabeled commit can never accidentally bump minor/major). The minter lives inside `deploy.yml` — the one context guaranteed to run once per alpha landing (a standalone `on: push` workflow is suppressed for GITHUB_TOKEN auto-merge pushes, and `deploy.yml` is already serialised per-branch, so it doubles as the race lock) — resolving the latest **reachable** tag (`git tag -l --merged HEAD`), classifying commits since it via the new standalone `classify-bump.sh`, cutting the next tag with existence+push guards, dispatching `release.yml` on the tag ref, and self-healing the CHANGELOG rollover in an isolated worktree. The promotion-bridge's unconditional minor-minting is retired (it would mint a phantom minor on a fixes-only promotion). Settings→About gains a **Build-number** row (the commit-SHA row is relabelled "Commit"). Retrospectively bumps the committed baseline **1.0.0 → 1.1.0** for the #1955 enhancement batch; a `v1.1.0` tag cut at the alpha tip after merge activates the scheme and surfaces `1.1.<build>`. Guards (rule #34/#35, mutation-proven): new `tests/test-bump-classifier.js` + extended `tests/test-versioning-pipeline.js`. PR #1964.

- feat(languages): **BCP 47 language registry — scheduled refresh, live-search picker, unknown-tag curation** — the #738 IANA/CLDR registry now keeps itself current and the picker actually suggests something:
  - **Scheduled refresh (M1)** — a new monthly GitHub Action (`.github/workflows/language-registry-refresh.yml`) silently keeps the registry current: Leg A refreshes the git-tracked snapshot files under `appWeb/.sql/data/` and pushes any diff straight to `alpha` (sanity-gated: File-Date header, a 99% record-count floor, valid CLDR JSON); Leg B independently pokes a new keyed endpoint, `/language-registry-refresh` (the `webhook-drain.php` shape — `X-Refresh-Key` header, `hash_equals`, 200/403/503/502 contract), which re-fetches upstream server-side and re-runs the idempotent #738 import against the shared DB. The endpoint is **dormant (503) until a human has pressed the existing #738 setup-database card once** — an unattended job must never be the thing that first runs schema DDL on the shared DB. The fetch/import core (`languageRegistryRefreshCore()`, `includes/language_registry_refresh.php`) is hoisted verbatim out of the existing admin "Refresh from IANA + CLDR" button, which is now a thin delegate — one mechanism, two callers.
  - **Live-search picker (M2/M3)** — the four-subtag IETF picker (`js/modules/ietf-language-picker.js`) no longer uses `<datalist>` at all: each of Language / Script / Region / Variant is now wired to the shared `iHymnsPlaceSearch.attach()` typeahead against four new public search actions (`?action=language_search|script_search|region_search|variant_search` on `/api`, backed by one shared `bcp47SubtagSearch()` core). This also fixes, at the structural root, a detached-DOM boot bug (#1907) where two of the picker's four dynamic builders (the v2 enrichment panel, the v1 inline per-line picker) booted on a still-detached subtree and silently never showed a suggestion — `attach()` never calls `getElementById()`, so it can't suffer that bug class at all. Free text still saves (a script subtag must never fail import), but is no longer silent about it: an inline amber note names any subtag that wasn't picked/matched, upgrading to "the server will reject it" when the composed tag also fails the grammar check client-side (a mirror of the server's own validator). The legacy `/manage/songbooks?action=script_search|region_search` admin endpoints stay live as aliases — links outlive code — now delegating to the same shared core instead of carrying their own copy.
  - **Unknown-tag curation (M4)** — a new "Unknown tags" panel on `/manage/languages?view=unknown` (gated `manage_languages` alone) lists every distinct language tag actually stored anywhere in the catalogue that's malformed, unregistered, or retired — derived live every visit (INFORMATION_SCHEMA + a few batched `GROUP BY`/`IN` queries), no new table. A type-the-count-to-confirm Remap rewrites a tag everywhere it's used: a plain bound `UPDATE` for most sources, the shared `lyricLinesWriteComponents()` write path (never a raw UPDATE) for the per-line `tblLyricLines.LanguageCode` override, FK/unique-key-aware handling for `tblSongTranslations.TargetLanguage`, and report-only (no rewrite) for user-typed `tblSongRequests.Language`.
  - Four new CI guards, all tree-derived and mutation-proven: `tests/php/test-language-registry-refresh.php` (the endpoint reads no input beyond its key, one `$sources` map, `hash_equals`, dormancy-gate ordering), `tests/test-bcp47-search-endpoints.js` (every emitted search action has a server case), `tests/test-ietf-picker-live-dom.js` (no boot-time `getElementById`, no `<datalist>` fingerprint anywhere in the module or its consumers), `tests/php/test-language-tag-audit.php` (the schema-derived source list + the classifier truth table). `tests/test-ietf-picker-detached-boot.js` was reworked in place (its old `<datalist>`-option-count assertions no longer apply once datalists are gone) to exercise the real `place-search.js` + `ietf-language-picker.js` integration end-to-end instead. No migration needed — the #738 registry already existed; this closes the automation, search, and curation gaps around it. Plan: `.claude/bcp47-language-registry-plan.md`.

- feat(security): **CAPTCHA provider-outage fallback + break-glass (#947/#340 follow-on)** — if the bot-protection provider ever has an outage, guarded forms no longer lock every real visitor out. The problem was an asymmetry nobody had stated: during an outage the shipped verify already **failed open for any request carrying a non-empty garbage token**, so a bot sending `captcha_token: "x"` sailed through — while a legitimate user whose widget never rendered had no token to send and was the *only* party actually refused. The fix is a **server-verified, self-closing grace window**: when this server's own outbound probes confirm the provider is unreachable (widget script and/or siteverify), gated forms fall back to the pre-CAPTCHA defence floor — per-IP / per-account budgets, honeypot and daily caps all still enforced — and normal enforcement resumes automatically on the first healthy probe. **The allow decision derives exclusively from server-side observations**: a browser can report that the widget failed to load, and that only makes the server *look* sooner; nothing a request says can ever make it *believe* (a client-asserted flag would be a universal bypass the moment a bot copied it). A **stale** "down" never admits — it forces a re-probe first, which is what makes the window self-closing rather than something that can wedge open. Probes are globally rate-limited (1 per 30 s, 2 s/3 s timeouts) and only ever run on the refusal path, so a healthy site pays **zero** outbound calls. Also new: **misconfiguration detection** — a mis-pasted secret key makes the provider answer `invalid-input-secret` on every check, which would refuse every real visitor forever with nothing visibly wrong; it is now detected, reported by name ("the provider is rejecting our secret key" — waiting will not fix it), and fails open meanwhile so a typo cannot brick the site. **Admin visibility**: a health strip on the CAPTCHA card with a "Check provider now" button, a Dashboard banner, and activity-log rows on state *transitions* only (never one per request — a busy outage must not flood the log). **Break-glass**: dropping an empty `CAPTCHA_DISABLED` file into the web-denied `includes/` folder over SFTP returns the whole feature to dormant, which is the only recovery from having guarded both sign-in doors when the challenge itself is broken; the check runs *before* any setting is read, precisely because the stored configuration is the thing that is wrong in that scenario. A per-form **"keep strict during an outage"** opt-out ships seeded empty (all guarded forms degrade open by default). `connect-src` also gained a conditional per-provider CSP append, carried as registry **data** rather than the previous unverified assumption that a widget's traffic always stays inside its own iframe — an assumption that is wrong for hCaptcha, and whose failure mode (a blocked XHR ⇒ a widget that never mints a token ⇒ every gated form refusing every real user) is indistinguishable from the very outage this work exists to survive. **Zero DDL** (two `tblAppSettings` rows), and **entirely dormant + byte-identical on an unconfigured install** — proven by execution in CI, not by inspection. Guarded by 137 assertions across `test-captcha-gate.php` (pure outage truth table, anti-bypass wiring, dormancy-by-execution, kill-file web-denial) and `test-captcha-client.js` (the hint is fire-and-forget and decision-inert); **21/21 mutations verified red** — including two that caught real bugs in the guards themselves on first run (a CSP check truncating at a PHP string concatenation, and an action-name check that stayed green against a typo because the correct name was a prefix of it — rule #34, twice, exactly as advertised).

- feat(search): **live typeahead suggestion dropdown on /search (#1936, rebuild of #307)** — the search page's box now shows a **quick-jump dropdown** of up to 8 matching song titles as you type; clicking one (or arrowing to it and pressing Enter/Tab) goes **straight to that song**. It sits alongside — never replaces — the full results list below (which keeps lyrics snippets, "Load more", sorting and the #1903 arrow-into-results keyboard nav). #307 ("autocomplete as you type") had been closed as superseded: its code belonged to the header search bar deleted in #812 and was removed as dead code because `_initAutocomplete` had **zero callers** — "built, reachable from nowhere". The owner asked any revival to be "a fresh, scoped issue against the current search page"; this is that rebuild, mounted from `initSearchPage()` and genuinely reachable. It **reuses** the existing `?action=search` read at a low limit (`?action=suggest` stays deleted — no new endpoint, no schema), navigates via the app's existing `[data-navigate]` click delegator, and gets its keyboard + full ARIA combobox behaviour from the **shared** `combobox-a11y` helper (rule #22), gated so it coexists cleanly with the #1903 nav. Enter with nothing highlighted still runs the full search (the #812 Enter-to-search fix is preserved). Guarded by a new tree-derived, mutation-proven `tests/test-search-typeahead.js` that asserts the whole reachability chain `router → initSearchPage → _initSuggest → panel` — the exact link #307 lacked. `b050eb19`. Closes #1936.

- fix(a11y): **theme-aware admin surfaces — 94 hardcoded `bg-dark` utilities removed (#1713)** — admin cards, panels, modals, accordions, form fields and status badges that were pinned to a dark background regardless of theme now follow the user's chosen theme (Light / high-contrast / System-light no longer show stray dark boxes). 1:1 swaps to Bootstrap 5.3 theme-following tokens (`bg-body-tertiary`, `text-bg-secondary`, …) across 19 `/manage/*` files, plus one public regression (the songbook page's "Find this songbook elsewhere" card). No intentionally-dark surface (projector overlay, share-card canvas) was touched; `border-secondary` (already theme-aware) preserved; no logic changed. `f83f731b`. Closes #1713.
- feat(ui): **full songbook NAME in the last two list-row sub-lines (#1531)** — the Home page's Popular/Recently-Viewed rows and a musician's discography rows now show the full songbook name ("Seventh-day Adventist Hymnal") instead of the bare code ("SDAH"), matching the search/favourites/history/set-list rows that already did. Home now uses the shared registry-backed resolver (covers every songbook, not just six hardcoded ones); the discography gets a new shared PHP helper `ihymns_songbook_name_label()` that is the server twin of the JS `songbookLabel()` (identical markup, one PHP↔JS↔CSS class-name lockstep guard). `d17a5095`. Closes #1531.
- fix(editor): **v1↔v2 editor parity — PublicId minted on the shared save core (#1744)** — a song created through the legacy whole-song save (or an importer) was left without its opaque `/s/<publicid>` permalink, while a v2 create minted one. The shared `editorSaveSongCore()` now mints it (gated, self-healing, never overwriting an existing one). The audit's other two findings were re-verified as already fixed (v2 credit dedup; legacy `NormalizedTitle`). `4e7d7d62`. Closes #1744.
- ci: **stop the Changelog false-red on protected branches + retarget Dependabot to alpha (#1891, #1892)** — the Changelog workflow's push-back to `beta` (a protected branch) was rejected by the ruleset, retried 5×, then failed — a cosmetic red on every beta push. It now treats a protected-branch rejection as an expected no-op (the update rides the promotion PR). Dependabot version-updates, which targeted `main`/`beta` (force-reset from alpha on every promotion, so their PRs were always overwritten), now all target `alpha`. `a88c2b01`. Closes #1891; the AGP-9 Android build-verify half of #1892 stays gated on a real Android toolchain.

- feat(editor2): **field-level revision blame + per-field revert (#1122)** — the Song Editor's Revisions tab gains a **"Field history"** view alongside the existing time-ordered History: one row **per field** showing *who last changed it and when* — so "who changed this copyright line?" no longer means scrolling through unrelated edits — plus a per-field **Revert** that undoes just that one field (writing a normal new edit, never rewriting history) without discarding every other change made since. Pure read/compute over the existing `tblSongRevisions` (no schema): a new `revision_snapshots` bulk read serves the whole history, and a tested pure walk (`blameFromSnapshots`) attributes each field's last change, tolerant of the **three snapshot shapes** stored across the app's history (it folds a 2022 lowercase `title` and a 2024 `Title` into the same field, and never confuses a field *absent* in an older shape with one that was *cleared*). Revert is offered only where it's meaningful — a genuinely-changed, non-derived field (songbook / has-audio / has-sheet-music are excluded), reusing the editor's existing single-field save path (allow-list, CSRF, entitlements all inherited). Structured-group (sections/credits/tags) blame + rollback are a documented fast-follow; whole-revision Restore already covers "the lyrics are wrong, go back". `6044a572`→`301c32b0`. Design: `.claude/revision-blame-1122-design.md`.

- feat(webhooks): **partner-event outbound webhooks platform (#1909)** — external systems (partner churches, sibling projects, presentation-software integrators) can subscribe to iHymns events and receive **signed HTTP POST callbacks** with retry, dead-lettering and an admin triage surface. Events cover the catalogue (`song.*`, `songbook.*` incl. `songbook.import_completed`), sharing (`setlist.shared`) and live services (`service.started`/`.ended`); payloads are **identity + metadata only, never content** (no lyrics, media, share tokens or join codes — partners fetch content through the gated read API). Delivery is a durable outbox: a near-instant first attempt runs post-response in the emitting request, a key-authed `/webhook-drain.php` endpoint (cron / uptime monitor) progresses retries on a 2.2-day jittered backoff ladder, and a bounded piggyback keeps a cron-less install moving. Signatures are Stripe-compatible (`t=…,v1=…`, HMAC-SHA256 over `t.body`, with a dual-signature rotation grace); the outbound dialer is SSRF-hardened for an attacker-controlled target (https-only, DNS pre-resolution + a private/reserved-range truth table over every resolved address incl. IPv4-mapped IPv6, an IP pin defeating rebinding, no redirects, a self-host block). Endpoints must pass a **challenge-echo verification** before receiving anything. New `/manage/webhooks` admin surface (`manage_webhooks`, global-admin) to register/verify/pause/rotate-secret/test/re-drive + a delivery log and recent-events view; a `/manage/configuration` card is the per-channel master switch + drain-key custody. **Entirely dormant** — three tables, one migration, all additive/idempotent; nothing emits or delivers until `webhooks_enabled_channels` names a channel, verified a byte-identical no-op while off. A 7-agent adversarial security review (SSRF / secret-custody / dormancy / emit-safety lenses) confirmed 3 findings: two fixed in the same batch (an SSRF blind spot on NAT64/6to4/IPv4-compatible IPv6 that embed a private/metadata v4; and the editor-save emit moved out of the save transaction to post-commit, closing a possible silent false-success), one filed as #1934 (the design's accepted §A.14 secret-re-wrap gap). `e7878004`→`aea3c28e` (follow-ups #1933, #1934).

- feat(themes): **browse-by-theme at scale (#1148)** — a new searchable **`/themes` A–Z index** replaces the home page's old one-click "Browse all themes" reveal (which re-created the unbounded chip wall it was meant to remove). Every theme is grouped A–Z with a type-to-filter box and a letter jump bar (all client-side, no extra network calls); each row links to the existing `/tag/<slug>` page. The home "Popular themes" strip's counts — and the new index's — now show the **visible-song count** (soft-deleted songs and disabled songbooks excluded) via one shared count core, so a chip's number always matches the page it opens (previously they could disagree). Theme surfaces also gained the SEO they were missing: `/themes` + every `/tag/<slug>` in the sitemap, dedicated social-preview cards, proper browser titles (the per-theme pages had none), and a "Themes" entry in the main menu. Zero schema change — reads existing tables, correct on fresh/un-migrated/migrated installs alike. `9d436c1b`→`99c4e7f8`.

- feat(security): **account-security pack (#1027 / #947 / #340)** — a per-account login lockout on both login doors, plus an opt-in, provider-agnostic CAPTCHA that ships entirely dormant:
  - **#1027** — the brute-force lockout that counts bad guesses *per account* (not just per IP, so a botnet giving each address only a few tries can no longer grind one password) now protects the **/manage admin login** too — it previously guarded only the public API login, leaving the highest-value accounts open to exactly the attack it was built to stop. Both doors now share **one** allowance via a single key derivation, so splitting guesses across `/api` and `/manage` no longer buys two budgets. `00bb4f72`.
  - **#1927** — the email-login-code request endpoint gained a per-IP flood limit (it had none), so one address can no longer trigger real emails to an unbounded list of strangers. `f6cddecc`.
  - **#947 / #340** — an admin can now switch a "prove you're human" challenge (**Cloudflare Turnstile, hCaptcha or Google reCAPTCHA v2**) onto sign-up, sign-in, password-reset, email-login and the song-request forms, from a new card on `/manage/configuration`. Verified server-side (the secret key never reaches a browser, and is encrypted at rest); the per-IP / per-account / per-identifier rate limits stay in force underneath it. **Entirely dormant and byte-identical until an admin picks a provider, pastes both keys, and ticks a form** — an unconfigured install (every install today) behaves exactly as before, down to the CSP header and the API responses. Which provider to activate — and whether visitor interaction data may leave the site — is an owner call; the mechanism is provider-agnostic (a self-hosted proof-of-work option can be added without touching the gates). `a962319f`→`9289c23e`.

- feat(branding): **#1840 org logos on three screen surfaces** — the #1830 logo registry now shows up beyond print: a small **org emblem co-brand** in the app's top header (progressive-enhancement, signed-in members only, theme-matched — light/dark logo variants can now be uploaded); a **corner-bug logo** on the Service-Projection screen (top-left, low-opacity, an operator on/off toggle remembered per projection device, default ON); and a **branded colour band** on a shared set-list's social-preview card (a new per-org `BrandColor`, the org's reversed/emblem PNG logo or its name, a "via iHymns" credit) — gated on the link's existing "show who shared this" consent, never a forced disclosure. All three are dormant until an org actually uploads/sets something; print output is byte-identical (the variant-aware admin card's fold gained a `'default'`-only filter before the light/dark upload UI could ever create a non-default row). One new pure resolver (`ihymnsOrgLogoResolveThemedAsset()` + its JS twin) drives every themed surface's kind/variant fallback ladder. `7b627adf`→`96f1b507`.
- perf: **Wave 3 perf & resilience pack (#1920, #1921, #1571 safe subset)** — the app's heaviest cold-path reads get a cache, a conditional-revalidation shortcut, and an honest consent+progress affordance:
  - **#1920** — `/qr.php` and the server-PDF renderer's inline QR image no longer round-trip the CueRCode service for a picture that never changes. A new server-side `tblQrCache` (dormant, additive schema) is read through the ONE CueRCode client via `cuercodeGenerateCached()`; a hit skips the network call entirely, a miss falls through to the untouched HTTP path and caches only a real success (never a failure, so an outage can't freeze into a permanent cached 503). Dormant until the CueRCode key is configured; a 90-day TTL + 20,000-row belt bound growth. `45f9232e`→`77225ba0`.
  - **#1921** — the PWA's slim catalogue index (`?action=songs_index`) is no longer re-sent in full on every fetch. The server now answers a matching `If-None-Match` with a **304 and no body**, skipping the ~14.5k-row query entirely — keyed on a version-signal ETag (two cheap COUNT/MAX aggregates, corpus content + API contract version + deploy SHA + schema shape), never a hash of the payload itself. Crucially, the service worker also learned to actually SEND that validator — its `songs_index` route previously fetched with `cache: 'no-store'` for an unrelated (and still-needed) reason that also meant it never attached `If-None-Match`, which would have made the server-side half a silent no-op for the PWA's own offline index. `64560d43`→`967117ef`.
  - **#1571 (safe subset)** — `songbook_export` gets its OWN 60/min rate-limit bucket, split from the `bulk` budget it used to share with the offline-sync endpoints, so a curator's export and a native app's background sync can no longer contend for the same counter. Every export surface (the public song/songbook views, the `/songbooks` list, the Song Editor) now asks before building a 500+ song export ("this may take a few minutes"), and the ProPresenter bundle builder reports coarse 20%-step progress while it works, cooperatively yielding every 25 songs so the page stays responsive instead of looking hung. The full chunked-fetch/streaming re-architecture remains a surfaced owner decision — #1571 stays open. `804a9700`, `48c48de9`.

- fix(setlist): **set-list sync correctness cluster (#1662 / #1675 / #1660 / #1802)** — four related fixes to how set lists sync and share:
  - **#1662** — a set list with more than 200 songs was silently amputated to 200 on sync, with no flag. It is now a **413 rejection** on all three write paths (nothing stored), never a silent slice; the response carries a machine-readable `reason` (`too_many_songs` / `body_too_large` / `too_many_slots`) so clients branch on status + reason, not prose. Legacy native apps keep today's truncation until they adopt sync protocol 2 (#1923). `3dd54670`.
  - **#1675 / #1660** — the sync upsert was unconditional last-writer-wins (and its doc-comment claimed the opposite). It now runs a **per-row conflict guard**: a stored row newer than the client's watermark is kept, not overwritten, and reported in a new `conflicts` response array; the client takes the server copy and re-applies. A one-clock alignment + a future-stamp clamp make the compare frame-safe; an identical push is a no-op (no spurious UpdatedAt bump). `99a75d77`, `f4939873`.
  - **#1802** — the shared set-list edit surfaces (token link + account collaborator) now have an **add-a-song** search box, not just reorder/remove — one shared accessible combobox over the public `?action=search` read, writing through the existing paths. `be24fd3f`.
- feat(export): **#1912** — exporting a song through the interchange/songbook bundle and re-importing it now keeps its **alternative titles**, which were previously dropped on a round-trip. `getSongs()` now emits `alternativeTitles`; the iHymns-JSON importer maps the field back through the shared `song_alt_titles.php` write core (schema + fixture updated to match). `585eaa78`.
- feat(search): **#1903 (item 2)** — keyboard navigation on `/search`: pressing ArrowDown from the search box now moves focus into the results list, arrow keys move between results, and Enter opens the focused one. A delegated keydown handler on the persistent results container plus an input-level ArrowDown hook — not a typeahead revival. (Item 1, synonym search, remains a deferred owner-ask.) `fc37dcaf`.
- feat(editor2): **#1628 (items 3+4)** — the Song Editor's Revisions tab now shows a **field-level diff** of what a revision changed before you restore it (`revision_get` endpoint + a pure `diffSnapshots()` that handles v2/legacy/null snapshot shapes), and the **bulk toolbar can now Move, Delete and Export** several selected songs at once, not just verify/tag (api2 `bulk_move`/`bulk_delete` returning per-song verdicts over the shared relocate/delete cores; editor2 bulk bar + client plumbing; export via bounded per-id reads per rule #17). `da5b6f6f`, `2b6b2242`.
- feat(import): **#1911** — ZIP bulk-import gained a **dry-run / preview-only** mode: ticking it before uploading shows what would be created or skipped without writing anything. `tblBulkImportJobs.DryRun` persists the flag across the async job boundary; column-gated so an un-migrated install keeps its existing 422 refusal. `09cb0453`.
- feat(editor2): **#1900** — a song can now have **multiple copyright holders**: the Copyright Holder control in the Song Editor is an ordered add/remove/reorder chip list, with the primary (first-listed) holder still driving the displayed copyright line. `tblSongCopyrightHolders` M:N + a single-writer shared core (denorm re-sync) + api2 list/replace + chip-list UI; dormant until its migration card is applied. `41aed076`, `fa4a2149`.
- feat(medley): **#1907 (#1860 Phase 5)** — wires the dormant #1860 work-identity schema and adds custom section labels. A curator can give any song section a custom **display** name (e.g. "Kyrie", or one-click its language name) in place of the automatic "Verse 1 / Chorus" — new `tblSongComponents.Label`, **display-only** so the section `Type` still drives chorus highlighting, slide grouping and every machine-export keyword (a label never reaches an exporter). A Work can be defined as an ordered list of constituent Works (a **medley**) via a new "Constituent works" editor on `/manage/works` (`tblWorkComponents`), so the song page and `/work/<slug>` show a read-only "Medley of: A, B, C"; each song section can point at the Work it excerpts (`tblSongComponents.SourceWorkId`), which additively records the medley composition. One shared medley write core (`workMedley*()` in `includes/work_admin.php`); label/link are thin-row metadata on the component save funnel (never the lyric-line path); the write path is silent-wipe-proof across all funnels; a tree-derived mutation-proven guard (`tests/test-component-label-sites.js`) keeps every display surface honouring the label while exporters stay structural. Per-section language was already live (#858/#1206). No behaviour change on an un-migrated install (every new read/write is column-gated). 10 commits `417a9160`→`734b6f29`.
- feat(ilid): **#1860 go-live A/B/C** — `includes/ilyrics_id.php` mints a permanent, grammar-disjoint internal id (`IL<letter><10 digits>`, e.g. `ILS0000012345` — no separator, so it can never collide with the public `<letters>-<digits>` SongId) on every entity create, across every write funnel (both editors, importers, lyrics ingest, the `/manage` CRUD pages) (go-live A). Work auto-link now runs on every song save via one fail-safe wrapper in `includes/work_admin.php` — a song's Work membership stays in sync automatically instead of requiring a manual pass (go-live B). Every ILID-bearing lookup now accepts EITHER address form: `song_detail`/`song_data`'s `id`, `?page=musician`/`publisher`/`tune`'s `slug`, and `/song-media/<id>` all resolve an IL id to the record it names, gated on the migrated `IlId` column so an un-migrated install behaves exactly as before (go-live C).
