That renderer is **escape-first** — it `htmlspecialchars()` the entire input *before* transforming a strict subset (headings, bold, code, scheme-allow-listed links, lists) — which an adversarial pass then failed to defeat across 27 vectors including `javascript:`/`data:`/protocol-relative URLs, attribute-break attempts and HTML nested inside every markdown construct. Alongside it, two environment affordances: the existing env badge (#812) is now **colour-distinct** (Alpha amber / Beta blue — they were identical, so two open tabs were indistinguishable), and both the badge's dropdown and the footer version now link through to What's New. Environment clarity is not cosmetic here: Live Follow sessions are walled per environment, and a cross-environment join fails with only a generic "wrong code" toast (#1339). One bug was caught in review and fixed before merge — passing `ENT_QUOTES` explicitly to `htmlspecialchars()` **drops** the `ENT_SUBSTITUTE` that PHP 8.1+ supplies by default, so a byte-capped excerpt cut mid-UTF-8-character rendered as a completely **blank card**; measured at 69 of 10,000 sampled cut points, and guaranteed on the first deploy since the real CHANGELOG (72,694 bytes) exceeds the cap.
- feat(web): **global JavaScript error surfacing** (#1582). The PWA had **zero** `window.onerror` and **zero** `unhandledrejection` handlers — every uncaught error died console-only. That is the precondition that let the public Export feature stay broken for seven weeks (#1565), and it recurred twice more in the same session (#1568, #1576). A new `js/modules/error-monitor.js`, booted before anything else in `app.js`, catches uncaught errors and rejections, shows one generic toast, and beacons a deduplicated one-liner to a new `client_error_report` endpoint. Defences that matter: a **three-layer throttle** (per-fingerprint once per 10 min persisted in `sessionStorage` so a crash-reload loop cannot re-beacon on every reload; a 10-per-page cap; a token bucket) verified to hold under 50-error bursts; a **re-entrancy guard** so an error thrown while showing the toast can never re-enter the handler; and a **privacy scrub** stripping bearer tokens, 64-hex strings and query secrets from both the client and the server side, with stack frames reduced to `pathname:line`. **No new table and no migration** — it reuses `logActivity()` and `tblActivityLog`, which already carry environment, route, user-agent and a JSON details column; review it at `/manage/activity-log` filtered to `Action=client.jserror`. Deliberately NOT routed through `analytics.js`, which is consent-gated: operational error telemetry that vanishes for consent-declined users would recreate the exact blindness this exists to remove.
- fix(web): **Settings language filter silently never refreshed Song of the Day** (#1581). DOM event names are case-sensitive, and three modules disagreed: `settings-language-filter.js` dispatched `ihymns:language-filter-changed` while the only listener, `song-of-the-day.js`, wanted `iHymns:…` — a spelling only `songbook-language-filter.js` emitted. So changing the filter from a songbook page worked and changing it from Settings did nothing, with no error. Two further dead wires were found in the same census: `offline-ui.js` listens for an `ihymns:offline-settings-changed` that nothing dispatches (which is also why the "include audio offline" preference is inert for tile downloads), and the editor dispatched an `iHymns:languages-loaded` nobody listened for (removed). Rather than patch the casing, every event name now lives once in `js/constants.js` — the module that already centralises storage keys "to prevent key-name drift (#139)" — and a new `tests/test-event-names.js` **bans raw `ihymns:*` literals outside that file** and asserts every name has both a dispatcher and a listener, so this class cannot recur.
- docs(live): **Live Follow and Service Mode user documentation** (#1577) — two adjacent in-app help topics (`help.php`'s "Following a Live Service (Service Mode)" / "Go Live — Lead Your Own Live Session (Live Follow)"), a new top-level `help/live-follow.md` guide, `wiki/Live-Follow-&-Service-Mode.md`, and a Live Sessions section in Apple's native `HelpView`. The two features share a lookalike code and join dialog but are started completely differently (any signed-in user from a song, vs. a church admin from the admin console) — every doc conflated them, which is why Live Follow looked permanently broken when it was actually being driven with Service Mode's prerequisites. All four now lead with an explicit "they are different features" comparison.
- fix(web): **the public Export menus now actually work — they never had** (#1565, #1566, #1567, #1568, #1570; tests #1569). A user reported that Export ▾ → ProPresenter 7+ did nothing on both a single song and a whole songbook. It turned out **no format had ever worked on any public page** since the feature shipped: the only code binding those menu items was a nonce-less inline `<script>` inside the song/songbook fragment, and the document sends an **enforcing nonce CSP** (#117) that refuses it. Bootstrap still opened the dropdown, so the menu looked alive while nothing was bound — hence the silent no-op with not even an error toast (the "Preparing export…" toast is the first statement *inside* the never-attached listener). Born broken: the CSP shipped 2026-04-06 (`a21ea99f`), the export UI 2026-06-07 (`5dedf5d4`) — nothing regressed, the two simply never met. Because the fragments are **shared-cache** responses (`$_cacheablePages`) they can never carry a per-request nonce (rule #6), so the fix is the pattern this repo had already established for `home.php`: wiring moved into real ES modules that the **SPA router** imports after the fragment loads, with IDs sourced DOM-first from `.page-song[data-song-id]` / the new `.page-songbook[data-songbook-abbr]` (the record id, correct even when the route used an alias, #1343-B) and the route param as fallback. The same cause had silently killed the **Present** button (#297) — extracted to `js/modules/present-mode.js` in the same pass, fixing a leaked document `keydown` handler on close while there (the old code listened for `'remove'`, which is not a DOM event, so the cleanup never ran). Behind the CSP break sat a **second, fully masked bug** (#1566): `propresenter-export.js`'s proto-bundle URL was document-relative, so from `/song/MP-1008` it fetched `/song/protos/proto-bundle.json` — which the SPA catch-all answers **HTTP 200 with HTML**, passing the `response.ok` guard and throwing inside `.json()`. Now root-absolute; the `bundleUrl` override is preserved. Also fixed: editor v2 loaded `propresenter-export.js` without the protobuf runtime so PP7 export always failed there (#1567); the single-song PP7 export was un-`await`ed so post-init failures became unhandled rejections with no toast; and the two hand-maintained format menus (which had drifted — songbooks silently lacked **ChordPro**, while a dead `FORMATS` constant in `export-ui.js` lacked ProPresenter 7+) are now one shared `includes/partials/export-menu.php` (#1570). **Why nobody caught it:** the ProPresenter suite passed 54/54 while injecting the bundle straight into `init()`, so the runtime fetch path had never once executed; `export-ui.js` had zero coverage; and CI ran no node tests at all. #1569 closes all of that — a new `tests/php/test-fragment-inline-scripts.php` CI guard bans executable nonce-less inline `<script>` in any SPA fragment (comment-stripping so `home.php`/`person.php` mentions don't false-positive; a count-exact self-cleaning allowlist for the one remaining case, #1572), plus a bundle-URL regression test that exercises the real fetch, a first-ever `export-ui.js` smoke suite, and the node tests wired into CI. Every new test was **proven able to fail** against the pre-fix tree before being accepted.
- feat(apple): **App Intents / Siri / Shortcuts + Spotlight catalogue search** (Apple Phase-1.5, #1415). The native app becomes a first-class citizen of the system surfaces: four **App Intents** — Open a Song, Play the Song of the Day, Go Live (host a service), Join a Service by code — exposed to **Siri + the Shortcuts app** via an `AppShortcutsProvider`, plus **Spotlight indexing** of the whole song catalogue so a hymn is findable straight from system search and a result tap deep-links into it. Built entirely on existing seams rather than new machinery: the intents drive the app through the SAME `DeepLinkRouter`/`AppNavigationState` path a Universal Link or Handoff continuation uses (via a new `pendingDeepLink` mailbox), `OpenSong`'s `SongEntity` query REUSES the one shared `songsMatching` matcher (never re-forked), and a Spotlight-result tap round-trips through the identical resolve seam. A `SpotlightIndexer` (in IHAppSupport, framework-neutral core + a `#if canImport(CoreSpotlight) && !os(tvOS)` live path — the only correct gate, since watchOS has no CoreSpotlight and tvOS prohibits its types) reindexes only when a SHA-256 corpus fingerprint changes or every 14 days, batched over the ~14k songs; the trigger also finally wires the Phase-1 `OfflineStore.upsert` resync path that had shipped dormant, so Siri entity resolution works offline. Content-gating-safe: intents/entities/Spotlight carry SongID/titles/codes only — never lyric text (a Lock-Screen/Spotlight-visible surface). Sign-in-gated where it must be (Go Live); anonymous where the web is (Join by code). No new entitlement or `project.yml` change. +36 Swift-Testing tests on the pure cores (Spotlight entry/fingerprint/indexer batching + tap round-trip, the intent→route map, the SongEntity resolver, the catalogue→cache→index sync); `swift test` green, iOS + watchOS + tvOS package cross-compiles green, SwiftLint 0, and the macOS app-scheme build's `appintentsmetadataprocessor` extracts the intent metadata cleanly. **Device-only (owner verifies):** real Siri phrase matching, the Shortcuts listing, Spotlight surfacing + result taps, cold-launch intent resolution. Full design: `.claude/apple-native-strategy.md` §2.3#4.
- fix(apple): **accessibility sweep across the Phase-2 live/remote surfaces** (#1562) — VoiceOver quick-wins found in review: `.accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` was swallowing the destructive "Stop Mirroring"/"Disconnect" buttons into their status-text group (stripping the button trait + destructive role) on `ServiceMirrorCard` and `RemoteControlSurfaceView`; the tvOS follow surface's only "Leave" control lived in a tap-to-reveal strip undiscoverable under VoiceOver; live-status changes (mirror phase, tvOS join errors, watch wake/failure notices) were silent instead of announced; and placeholder-only text fields lacked real accessibility labels.
- feat(apple): **Live Activities + Dynamic Island (now-singing) + the APNs push-bridge** (Apple Phase-2 PR-16, #1429). A "now-singing" Live Activity on the Lock Screen + Dynamic Island while a host drives a live session — current song / section / live-state, with **Next/Prev App-Intent buttons** for the host — kept fresh in the background by the server pushing updates over APNs. Two verifiable halves + a device-only tip. **Backend (verified):** a new shared `includes/live_activity_push.php` fires a push on every session state-change and end (hooked into all six broadcast/end sites — `live_follow_update`/`service_broadcast`/`*_leave`/`*_end` + the two supersede paths), building Apple's ActivityKit `aps` envelope from navigation intent ONLY (song title + section + displayState — **never lyric text**); **entirely dormant** until an APNs key is provisioned (new `/manage/configuration` card) AND a device registers a live-activity token — a verified no-op otherwise. A security fix: a `kind=liveActivity` `apns_register` now requires host/operator authorisation (was: any authenticated user against any active sessionId). **Client (verified to compile + unit-test):** a new `IHLiveActivity` iHymnsKit target — the `NowSingingActivityAttributes`/`ContentState` wire contract (matching the PHP `content_state` byte-for-byte against one shared fixture both languages test), pure reducer/section-navigator/intent-bridge cores, and the iOS-only ActivityKit controller + `LiveActivityIntent`s + the lock-screen/Dynamic-Island UI (all `#if os(iOS)`-gated so watchOS/tvOS/macOS still build); the `AppRootViewModel` drives it from the existing host event flow (never a second stream consumer), registers the activity's push token (scoped to the session, never logged), and `hostAdvanceSection` powers the Next/Prev intents. No schema change (`tblApnsTokens` #1410 was forward-looking). +37 Swift-Testing tests + a 57-assertion PHP suite; `swift test` green (874), iOS + watchOS package cross-compiles green, SwiftLint 0, `php -l` clean tree-wide. **Device-only (owner verifies):** the actual Live Activity start/update/end, Dynamic Island rendering, the real APNs push round-trip, and the Next/Prev buttons need a physical iPhone + a provisioned APNs `.p8` key + the Push Notifications capability — a device-matrix is on #1429. Full design: `.claude/apple-native-strategy.md` §2.3.
- feat(apple): **tvOS server-following projector — join a live Service session on the TV** (Apple Phase-2 PR-15, #1428). The multi-site/overflow + **LAN-fail degradation rung**: when the direct LAN TV remote can't reach the screen (AP isolation / VPN / firewall) or the screen isn't on the venue LAN at all, an Apple TV can instead **follow the Service session over the internet** — a new **Live** tab where the operator enters the venue join code and the TV mirrors whatever the leader is showing (song / section / blackout / logo), polling ~1 s. It reuses the whole existing stack rather than forking any of it: the PR-10 `ServiceModeEngine` + `LiveFollowerReducer` follower spine (gaining only a defaulted `role: .projector` — the server's #1406 projector budget of 90/min + `pollIntervalMs:1000`, already live on alpha, now also **persisted across a cold-start resume**), and the PR-5 `ProjectionViewModel`/`ProjectionCanvasView` render surface (the projector is simply its THIRD driver, after the Siri Remote and the LAN remote). The bridge between them, **`TVServiceFollowBridge`**, is a pure snapshot→projection-plan mapper mirroring PR-6's `TVProjectionBridge` (deduped display-state + song/section diff; maps the server `live|blackout|logo` onto the projection vocabulary, never `.frozen`; a legacy unparsable songId becomes a calm "can't be displayed" notice, never a crash), and **`TVServiceFollowCoordinator`** owns its OWN second `ServiceModeEngine` instance (the single-consumer rule) + a pure `ServiceModeEvent→FollowPhase` table. **Content-gating-safe by construction:** the sync wire carries navigation intent only — never lyric text — and the TV fetches each song under its OWN auth, so gating (and any licensed-content unlock) applies on the TV exactly as in a browser; an internet drop holds the last projection and resumes. No new backend (the #1406 role/budget/interval were already on alpha). +35 new Swift-Testing tests (the pure bridge mapping/dedupe/D-13 matrix, the pure phase table, projector role/cadence-encode + resume-persistence + legacy-record decode); `swift test` green (872), iOS + watchOS package cross-compiles green, SwiftLint 0 violations. Full design: `.claude/apple-native-strategy.md` §2.4.7.
- feat(apple): **optional Service-Mode mirror — the LAN TV remote can also drive the web congregants** (Apple Phase-2 PR-14, #1425). The bridge between the two deliberately-separate live features (strategy §2.4.1): the tvOS app owns the ONE canonical `(songId, componentIndex, displayState)`, a LAN remote command changes it, and — **only when the operator opts in** — that state is also mirrored to a running Service Mode session via the EXISTING `service_broadcast` endpoint so remote congregants stay in sync too. **Zero new backend** (the native call matches the web operator consoles' request byte-for-byte, server-authorised by the operator's own bearer via `serviceMode_userCanOperate`), and the LAN link never carries congregant traffic while the server never carries TV control — so an internet outage degrades ONLY the mirror, never the local projection. A new `@MainActor @Observable` `ServiceMirrorController` (injected broadcast closure — never touches `ServiceModeEngine`/`RemoteControlSession`/`RemoteSessionActor`/IHRP) taps the coordinator's `.controlling` echo (the TV's ACKED state — so mirroring on it IS "mirror-on-ack"), dedups on `songId|componentIndex|displayState` (lineIndex/revision excluded — the web congregant view has no line cursor), translates the LAN display vocabulary onto the narrower web one (`frozen→live`), and is single-flight + latest-wins-coalescing (never a queue). Failures never disrupt LAN control: `unauthorized`/403/404 stop the mirror with an honest notice, anything else degrades + retries the latest state once. The `ServiceMirrorCard` on the control surface is OFF by default and signed-in-gated — the operator types the session number (pre-filled from `IHSettingsStore`) and taps Start. Logs carry transitions + numeric session ids only (never a song id, join code, presence token, or bearer). Persistence helpers relocated into `RemoteControlCoordinator+Persistence.swift` for the LOC budget (the `+UIPhase.swift` precedent). +18 new Swift-Testing cases (controller dedup/coalescing/failure matrix via a gated spy, the `service_broadcast` wire shape, settings round-trip); `swift test` green, SwiftLint 0 violations. **Operator UX note:** the web operator consoles don't yet surface the numeric session id — a small display-only follow-up (owner decision) makes the number copy-able; until then it's read from the console URL. Full design: `.claude/apple-native-strategy.md` §2.4.1.
- feat(apple): **Apple Watch pocket remote — background wake-and-reconnect relay for the LAN TV remote** (Apple Phase-2 PR-11, #1423). The Watch drives the venue TV **while the iPhone is backgrounded / locked / in a pocket**: a reduced remote (Prev/Next verse, Lyrics/Black/Logo, honest status — strategy §2.4.2's set exactly) whose taps ride WatchConnectivity's documented background-wake (`sendMessage` from the watch launches iHymns in the background, locked-after-first-unlock included), where a new **`HeadlessRelayDriver`** — a second, process-lifetime session OWNER beside the screen-scoped `RemoteControlCoordinator` — reconnects the last-connected saved TV over PR-7's pinned fast path (saved token + pinned fingerprint + last-good address, <1 s typical, 4 s hard deadline), forwards the intent, replies synchronously, and idle-disconnects after a 20 s linger. **`Sources/IHLive/LANRemote/` carries ZERO diff** — no pairing/TOFU/Keychain/token-custody change; the headless driver is a new CALLER of the existing, security-reviewed session API, and a background burst that unexpectedly lands in a pairing ceremony is CANCELLED (`repairNeeded`), never completed. The seam is layered pure-first: a versioned watch↔phone wire protocol v1 (`WatchRelayCommand`/`WatchRelaySnapshot`/`WatchRelayReply` + a strict-version codec — install skew becomes honest "update" copy, never a decode crash), the pure `WatchRelayRules` route/merge tables (single-owner arbitration: the foreground coordinator always wins, and four awaited retire hooks stop any headless linger the instant a foreground connect starts), the pure `HeadlessRelayPolicy` wake/connect/linger state machine (a bounded ≤4 in-flight buffer, NO cross-wake queueing — a late-firing Blackout is a live-service hazard, so out-of-range is an honest failure, never a queue), the platform-neutral `RemoteControlRelayHub`, and two thin WCSession shells (`PhoneWatchRelayService` with `beginBackgroundTask` command + linger brackets and a force-disconnect expiration handler; `WatchRemoteController` + `WatchRemoteView`/`WatchRootView`, now the watch app's root). No new entitlement, background mode, plist key, or `project.yml`/`apple.yml` change; no credential ever crosses the watch link; no lyric text ever rides the relay (the snapshot is phase/tvName/songRef/displayState/revision only). +56 new Swift-Testing tests across 6 suites (codec round-trips + the <512-byte size guard, the full 36-cell route table + intent-map `isControlIntent` proof, every policy transition row with literal dates, snapshot mapping, hub arbitration, and a real-socket driver loopback E2E: wake-connect → forward → clean idle disconnect, warm path, in-order buffering, dead TV, revoked token, retire mid-linger, empty store); `swift test` green on host + iOS/watchOS package cross-compiles green, SwiftLint 0 violations. Device-matrix verification (real wakes, pocket/locked/BFU/force-quit rows) is captured honestly in the PR body per PR-6/7/8 precedent. Full design: `.claude/apple-phase2-pr11-pocket-spec.md`.
- feat(apple): **native Live Follow (leader + follower) + Service Mode congregant** (Apple Phase-2 PR-10, #1426, #1427). The SERVER-mediated live-worship-sync half of the native app — distinct from the peer-to-peer LAN TV remote (PR-4..8), sharing only the `IHLive` module, never code or protocol. A grown `LiveFollowEngine` actor hosts (`goLive`/`broadcast`, dedup on song+section, 30 s heartbeat + a scenePhase wake-beat, 409-on-broadcast → superseded) or follows (`join`/poll/leave, mutual exclusion with hosting — "host wins," web parity) a Live Follow session; a new `ServiceModeEngine` actor joins a venue's rotating code, polls, and holds the presence-token custody that (once the owner turns content gating on) quietly unlocks copyrighted lyrics for exactly the presence window — the token never rides an event payload, is persisted local-only (UserDefaults, never Keychain/synchronizable), and is logged only as an `IHLogSanitize` fingerprint. Both engines' follower halves drive ONE shared pure spine (`LiveFollowerReducer` — revision-monotonic dedup, the 180 s freshness window, the full server-declared/idle/backoff cadence table) so the two features can never quietly diverge on this logic. The presence-token → CCLI-unlock transport rides the SAME named cookie a browser congregant sends (`ihymns_sf_presence_token`, attached by `APIClient.makeURLRequest` whenever a shape-validated token is set, never a query param) — **zero backend change**. UI: `LiveHubView` replaces PR-7's honest "coming in a future update" placeholder with a real Join-with-a-Code sheet, a sign-in-gated "Go Live," a following screen (reusing the shared `SongComponentView`, never forking a second lyric renderer — content is fetched via the existing `song_detail` read, so nothing on the sync wire ever carries lyric text), a host console (`ShareLink` code sharing), and a bottom status banner on both root layouts. The native Service-Mode OPERATOR console (start/rotate/end a venue session) is deliberately out of scope — the web already serves it responsively, and its native consumers are already scheduled (PR-14/#1425, PR-15/#1428). +64 new Swift-Testing tests (reducer cadence table, mocked-transport engine loops, event→UI-state mapping, presence-Cookie transport rows); `swift test` green across macOS/iOS/tvOS/watchOS cross-compiles, SwiftLint 0 violations. Full design: `.claude/apple-phase2-pr10-spec.md`.
- feat(apple): **tvOS projection canvas + driver-agnostic projection engine** (Apple Phase-2 PR-5, #1504). The tvOS side of the LAN remote — the screen the congregation sees, and the "brain" that decides what's on it. A pure, total **`ProjectionResolver`** is the ONE place "what does *next* mean" is ever defined (relative-intent resolution with clamping, karaoke line-mode vs component-mode, rollover that skips empty components, `scroll` capped + terminating). A `@MainActor @Observable` **`ProjectionViewModel`** owns song fetching (under the TV's OWN auth, so content gating applies on the TV — the LAN link never carries lyric text), the canonical `(songId, componentIndex, lineIndex, displayState)` state, a latest-wins fetch guard, the frozen-snapshot rule (canonical keeps moving behind a freeze; remotes stay truthful), and a driver-facing `AsyncStream<ProjectionState>` whose 4-tuple **exactly matches PR-4's `TVListenerActor.updateState(...)`** — the seam PR-6 (#1421) bridges the LAN listener onto in ~30 lines. Plus **`ProjectionCanvasView`** (full-bleed lyrics/blackout/logo/frozen, reusing the shared `ihLyricLineStyle` typography seam + a karaoke current-line highlight) and **`TVRootView`** (Project · Songbooks · Search tabs) replacing the tvOS placeholder shell — reusing the EXISTING `SongbooksView`/`CatalogueListView` unmodified. The view-model is **driver-agnostic** (no `import Network`, no listener reference), so the Siri Remote (this PR), the LAN remote (PR-6) and a future server-follow projector (PR-15) all drive it without ever re-forking resolution logic. Compiling `IHFeatures` for tvOS **for the first time** surfaced (and fixed, minimally in-place with `#if os(tvOS)`) unavailable-API stragglers in 13 shared views (`ShareLink`/`.swipeActions`/`.draggable`/`DragGesture`/`DisclosureGroup`) — **iOS/macOS behaviour is byte-identical** (one, `SongMetadataView`, became a DRY improvement). **Apple-package + tvOS-shell only; dormant until PR-6 wires the LAN listener onto the seam.** +20 deterministic Swift-Testing tests (10 resolver + 10 view-model, injected fetcher — no network); `swift test` **575 green**, SwiftLint **0 violations**, and a new **tvOS Simulator build step** added to `apple.yml` (the shared library had never been CI-compiled for tvOS before). Full design: `.claude/apple-phase2-pr5-spec.md`.
- feat(apple): **IHLive/LANRemote — IHRP/1 remote-control protocol + TV/remote actors** (Apple Phase-2 PR-4, #1420). The peer-to-peer LAN transport a phone/iPad/Mac/Vision remote uses to drive the tvOS projector **directly** (no server round-trip): a versioned `Sendable`+`Codable` `IHRPMessage`/`IHRPFrame` wire vocabulary (carries ~200-byte **navigation intent only — never lyric text**; the TV fetches `song_detail` under its own auth), a pure length-prefixed **TLV framer** with a hard 4 KB/frame cap (worst-case buffering is bounded — an oversize declared length is dropped the instant its prefix is read, never buffered), a **`TVListenerActor`** (`NWListener`, TLS 1.3, `.unpaired → .pairing → .paired` per-connection confinement + a concurrent-unpaired-connection cap), a **`RemoteSessionActor`** (`NWBrowser` discovery + `NWConnection` connect with **pin-before-trust** self-signed P-256 certificate-fingerprint pinning, **fail-closed** on any mismatch → `.fault`), and **seq+timestamp last-writer-wins** for concurrent multi-remote conflicts. Pairing tokens are SHA-256-hashed at rest and **never logged verbatim**. Pairing *policy* (Keychain persistence + the 6-digit/QR ceremony) is deliberately deferred to PR-6 behind a small `LANRemotePairingAuthority` seam. **Apple-package only — no web/PWA/live surface; dormant until PR-5/PR-6 wire it into the app shell.** deterministic Swift-Testing suites that run on **every** CI runner (TLV framer caps + fuzz, IHRP JSON codec round-trips, pure last-writer-wins ordering, SHA-256 fingerprint known-answer vectors) plus **two environment-gated** suites a headless/unsigned `swift test` binary can't run and that CI cleanly **skips**: keychain-bridged **identity generation** (`lanRemoteKeychainIdentityAvailable()` — the login-keychain `SecIdentity` route returns `errSecItemNotFound` on the runner; forcing the file-keychain isn't an option since the same factory must run on iOS/tvOS) and the live-TLS-127.0.0.1 **loopback** integration (`IHYMNS_LAN_LOOPBACK_TESTS=1` — needs macOS Local Network permission). Both run on a dev Mac and on PR-6's signed on-device test target. `swift test` green, SwiftLint 0 violations, LOC-budget + privacy-manifest + no-secrets gates all pass.
- chore(version): **bump to 0.4000.0** — milestone marker for the Credit-People/admin + Apple Phase-2-foundation batch (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; README badge + table + OpenAPI `info.version` + help.php synced; PWA SW cache auto-syncs, #81). Up from `0.2750.0`. Highlights: **Credit People** — merge-target live-search typeahead (#1500), optional maiden-surname field + migration (#1501), link member-people to a Group person (#1502), bulk-promote cited-but-unregistered names to the registry (#1503), place live-search loading-state + full-hierarchy selection confirmation (#1507), whitespace-trim on identifier/URL entry (#1508); **Admin** — manual delete of a selected database backup with path-confinement + CSRF + confirm (#1509); a fix for a literal `</script>` in inline-script comments that broke Credit People (stray text + dead auto-detect) and the Service-Mode projection/lead pages (#1498); **Apple Phase 2 (foundation, dormant)** — live broadcast payload v2 with the load-bearing bidirectional `blank`↔`displayState` bridge (#1405), projector poll-budget + `pollIntervalMs` (#1406), and a one-pass forward-looking schema batch for the whole remote-control/device-pairing family (`tblAuthDeviceCodes`/`tblSessionControlTokens`/`tblApnsTokens` + `tblServicePresence.Role` + `tblApiTokens` device columns, #1407/#1408/#1409/#1410). Deferred to a follow-up (budget-paced): the Swift observability `IHLog` (#1505/#1506), the §3.2 Activity-Log lifecycle breadcrumbs, and the macOS DMG packaging (#1510).
- chore(version): **bump to 0.2750.0** — milestone marker for the Apple-readiness + hardening train landed on `alpha` this cycle (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; README badge + table + OpenAPI `info.version` synced to match; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81). Up from `0.2501.0`. Highlights since 0.2501.0: web Sign in with Apple activation docs + the Apple Provisioning Runbook expanded to full click-by-click TestFlight/App-Store steps with CarPlay entitlement **granted** and a Xcode-emulation testing walkthrough; a security fix redacting `presenceToken`/live-follow `code`+`token` from the Activity Log (CWE-532, #1492); an Apple Deploy CI kill-switch + doc-path excludes + platform-SDK install + export-compliance (#1494); and two owner-approval-pending strategy docs (remote-control observability, sequenced production-readiness). A full open-issue reconciliation closed 10 completed-but-open issues and status-annotated 67 more.
- chore(version): **bump to 0.2501.0** — the native Apple app release train (milestone 0.2500.0) plus secret encryption-at-rest (#1466) (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; README badge + table + OpenAPI `info.version` synced to match; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81). Up from `0.1321.0`.
- feat(apple): **native Apple Universal app — Phase 1 client + backend built** — the in-development native Apple app (one Universal app across iOS / iPadOS / macOS / tvOS / watchOS / visionOS, epic #895) reached Phase 1 web-app feature parity on the `feat/apple-universal` branch, together with the backend endpoints and Apple App Site Association (AASA) it depends on. The branch has not been merged to `alpha` or deployed to any live environment.
- feat(auth): **Sign in with Apple** (`?action=auth_apple`, #1402) — verifies the Apple identity-token JWT against Apple's published JWKS and mints the standard iHymns bearer token.
- feat(auth): **self-service account deletion** (`?action=account_delete`, #1403) — bearer-authed, re-authenticated deletion of the caller's own account, satisfying App Store Review Guideline 5.1.1(v) for the native app.
- feat(apple): **in-app account deletion UI** (#1477) — the native app now surfaces a re-authentication-gated "Delete Account" flow (Password or Email-Code, reusing the login UI) that calls `?action=account_delete`, satisfying App Store Review Guideline 5.1.1(v) **in the client** (#1403 was backend-only; `AccountView` previously offered only Sign Out). A wrong/expired re-auth credential (401), rate-limit (429), or last-Global-Admin block (409) leaves the session untouched; only a confirmed 200 clears local token + favourites + setlists + offline caches, exactly like sign-out. +5 Swift-Testing files; `swift test` 514 green, SwiftLint clean.
- feat(analytics): **first-party analytics ingest** (`?action=analytics_ingest`, #1448) — anonymous, consent-gated event batch from native app clients; no user id, device id, or IP address is stored.
- feat(pwa): **admin-configurable native app store IDs + native-app install banner** (#1462) — a global admin can set an App Store / Google Play / Amazon Appstore listing at `/manage/configuration`, which replaces the PWA "Add to Home Screen" prompt with a native-app download banner on that platform once configured. Blank by default; no store listing exists yet.
- feat(security): **secret encryption-at-rest — full P1–P5 build, readers-first, dormant** (#1466). The 8 secrets the running app keeps in `tblAppSettings` (SMTP password, SendGrid/Mailgun/SES keys, Azure Graph client secret, Gmail service-account JSON, and the Sign-in-with-Apple `.p8`) get a self-describing `enc:v1:<alg>:<keyid>:<nonce>:<ciphertext+tag>` envelope — libsodium `crypto_secretbox`, or AES-256-GCM fallback — from `includes/secret_crypto.php`, decrypted at runtime with a master key held in `appWeb/.auth/secrets_master_key.php` (sibling of the docroot, git-ignored, never web-served). A DB dump/backup or a SQL-injection then yields only ciphertext; disclosure needs **two** primitives (a DB read **and** a filesystem read of the key). **Everything is DORMANT and a verified no-op** — every value stays legacy plaintext until a master key is provisioned on all 3 docroots **and** the gated encrypt-in-place migration runs. Shipped in one branch:
  - **P1** — engine + decrypt-capable readers (`EmailService.php`, `getAppSetting()`) + fail-closed encrypt-on-save (`manage/configuration.php`).
  - **P2** — `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` seeds the master key from the `SECRETS_MASTER_KEY` GitHub Secret **only if absent** (idempotent, 64-hex-validated, never clobbers a manual key; `.auth` mirror excludes it) + a Global-Admin **Secret-encryption panel** on `/manage/setup-database` (status, non-secret per-docroot key fingerprint, encrypted/legacy inventory, a decryptable **sentinel** health check, a per-docroot **beacon** parity table, and a "Generate master key" tool that displays a CSPRNG key once and **never writes `.auth/`**).
  - **P3** — the gated `manual`+`confirm=1` `migrate-secret-encrypt-inplace.php` (refuses unless a key is loaded **and** the cross-docroot sentinel is green) + a hardened **Rotate/re-encrypt** tool (password re-auth with brute-force lockout, type-to-confirm, and a **code-enforced cross-docroot keyid-parity gate** so a misordered rotation can't strand a lagging docroot; every decrypt audit-logged by key name only).
  - **P4** — `opcache-bust.php` now accepts the opcache key stored as a one-way `sha256:<hash>` (backward-compatible; the CI sender is unchanged).
  - **P5** — operator runbook in `DEV_NOTES.md`.
  Adversarially reviewed twice (23 findings fixed across both passes, incl. a `configuration.php` load-order fail-open that would have silently stored plaintext, keyid envelope-safety, and the rotation cross-docroot gate). CI guards: `tests/php/test-secret-crypto.php` (45 assertions) + `tests/php/test-secret-crypto-admin.php` (23). Does **not** protect a compromised runtime (RCE reads both). Full plan + honest threat model + operator runbook: `.claude/secret-encryption-strategy.md` / `DEV_NOTES.md`.

- fix(live): **align the two live-following systems' freshness + wake behaviour** (#1386 follow-up). The #1335 Service-Mode join/poll used tighter staleness windows than #1268 Live Follow (90 s join / 120 s poll vs 180 s), so a briefly-backgrounded projector tab could drop congregants or reject joins sooner. Both now share ONE `LIVE_SESSION_FRESHNESS_SECONDS = 180` constant (`includes/service_mode.php`) — 6× the 30 s broadcaster heartbeat. Also, the broadcaster's visibility/focus wake handler called `service_code_current`, which was **read-only**; it now refreshes `LastHeartbeatAt` too (operator-gated, active-only), so a projector waking from sleep immediately revives the session instead of waiting for the next 30 s code-rotate — mirroring Live Follow's wake-beat. No schema change. chore(version): bump to **0.1321.0**.

- fix(admin-login): **"Invalid form submission" lock-out when the login tab is left open** (#1386). Shared-hosting PHP session GC (`gc_maxlifetime` ≈ 24 min) reaps the *server-side* session while the browser cookie persists, so the baked CSRF token vanishes and a legitimate sign-in is rejected (incognito worked only because it's a fresh session). `login.php` now accepts a sign-in when EITHER the session token validates OR the POST is verifiably **same-origin** (`Origin`/`Referer` host matches `HTTP_HOST`) — the same robustness `validateCsrfRequest()` gives AJAX writes (rule #29), applied to the login form. Cross-origin login-CSRF is still rejected (foreign/absent `Origin`/`Referer`).
- fix(live-follow): **the home "Join a live service" button now also joins a worship-team "Go Live" session** (#1386). There are two distinct live-following systems — #1268 Live Follow ("Go Live" on a song → joined by the per-song "Join Live") and #1335 Service Mode ("Join a live service" on the home page) — and the prominent home button only matched Service-Mode (`SessionKind='service'`) sessions, so entering a "Go Live" code there always failed with "that code isn't active". The home join now transparently falls back to the Live-Follow join when no Service-Mode session matches the code, so the one discoverable button works for both. chore(version): bump to **0.1320.0**.

- fix(security): **deep second-pass security audit — auth brute-force, CSV-formula injection, admin-view stored XSS** (#1386 follow-up; deep adversarial sweep, 8 attack angles + verification). Fixed: (1) **admin-login brute-force** — `/manage` `attemptLogin()` now enforces the same IP lockout (10 fails / 15 min) the public `auth_login` has; the highest-value accounts were previously unthrottled; (2) **email-login-code brute-force → account takeover** — `auth_email_login_verify` (6-digit code, 10-min window) had no verification throttle; now caps failed code-verifies per IP; (3) **CSV / formula injection** (CWE-1236) in every admin export (activity-log, analytics, song export, CCLI report, editor skipped-IDs) — a new shared `includes/csv_safe.php` (`ihymns_fputcsv()`) prefixes leading `= + - @` so a malicious song title / search query / user-agent can't run as a formula on an admin's machine; (4) **stored XSS in admin views** — the credit-people merge-preview (link/IPI/ISNI free-text → `innerHTML`) and the editor revision-history username now escape via a quote-safe helper, and `admin_user_create` enforces the username charset allow-list that registration already used; (5) **IDOR** — `setlist_template_save` now verifies org membership before tagging a template to an org (mirrors the `setlist_schedule_save` fix); (6) `bulk_songs` `Cache-Control: private` when gating is on. Latent tier-cap bypasses + Service-Mode presence over-grant that only matter once `content_gating_enabled='1'` are tracked as enablement blockers in #1388. Also removed a duplicate `head-libs` include on the analytics page. chore(version): bump to **0.1319.0**.

- fix(security): **full adversarial security audit — XSS hardening, Host-header injection, IDOR** (#1386, clears CodeQL alert #3 on promotion to `main`). A 6-dimension sweep (input/SQL/XSS/CSRF/auth-IDOR/secrets+dangerous-fns); **SQL-injection, CSRF and secrets were CLEAN**. Fixed: (1) **DOM XSS** — the editor revision-history title + error message now `escapeHtml()` the `songId`/`err.message` (CodeQL #3); (2) **attribute-context XSS root cause** — the shared `js/utils/html.js` `escapeHtml()` used `textContent→innerHTML` which leaves `"`/`'` intact, so a curator/cross-user value could break out of a double-quoted attribute. It now escapes quotes, fixing the favorites/router/search/search-history/home-page findings in one shot; (3) **public stored XSS via `</script>` breakout** — `index.php` JSON-LD + `window.iHymnsConfig` and `person.php` JSON-LD add `JSON_HEX_TAG|_AMP|_APOS|_QUOT` so a DB song/songbook/person name can't close the `<script>`; (4) **Host-header injection / CWE-640 reset-link poisoning** — the password-reset, magic-link, email-verification and admin-notice email links, `getCanonicalUrl()`, and the gating-verifier probe now resolve the host via a new allow-list helper `appCanonicalHost()` instead of raw `$_SERVER['HTTP_HOST']`; (5) `analytics.php` CSV export strips `$_GET['export']` before `Content-Disposition`; (6) `setlist_schedule_save` **IDOR** — adds setlist-ownership + org-membership guards and fixes a phantom `CreatedBy` column (→`UserId`; it threw under STRICT). One LOW finding deferred (CF-Connecting-IP trust — needs a Cloudflare-IP-range infra decision, tracked in #1386). chore(version): bump to **0.1318.0**.

- chore(audit): **retire the legacy JSON-corpus Song Editor** (#1384) — a full-codebase audit for remaining snapshot/pre-MySQL patterns (follow-up to the #1380 setlist-snapshot fix) confirmed the bug class is otherwise closed (DB-direct reads hold everywhere). The one live hazard: `private_html/editor/` still wrote the entire ~3,600-song corpus to a frozen `songs.json` the live MySQL app never reads (silent data loss). Removed the file-writer backend + old UI; `index.php` is now a 301 redirect to the live `/manage/editor/`. chore(version): bump to **0.1317.0**.

- feat(live-follow): **NAT-safe join-token** (#1377) — a congregation behind one church-WiFi NAT is a single IP, so the per-IP poll rate-limit throttled the whole room. Live-Follow now mints a per-follower token at join and rate-limits polling **per token** (mirrors Service Mode), so a full room follows without being collectively throttled. Join cap raised 60→120/min.
- feat(sotd): **country-aware Song of the Day** (#1376) — adds national-civic themes gated on the user's country (US Thanksgiving 4th-Thu-Nov, Canadian Thanksgiving 2nd-Mon-Oct, US Independence Day), on top of the existing hemisphere awareness (broadened southern-zone coverage). No country / old client = verified no-op; liturgical themes stay global.
- fix(maintenance): **a `/manage`-logged-in admin can now use the main app** (#1382) — during maintenance, devs were stuck on the admin portal because `/manage` login only set a path-scoped session and never the cross-surface `ihymns_auth` token the bypass resolves. `/manage` login now mints that token (same shape as the main-app sign-in), so an admin is recognised across the whole site; logout revokes it. Cookie helpers extracted to a shared `includes/auth_cookie.php`. chore(version): bump to **0.1316.0**.

- feat(gating): **complete the dormant content-gating system** — uniform web/offline tier gating (#1357) + signed-URL sealing of licensed audio (#1358). Entirely **dormant + a verified byte-identical no-op** while `content_gating_enabled='0'` (the live state). #1357: the `song.php` HTML render (and the offline `bulk_songs` bundle that renders through it) now strip audio/sheet/MIDI affordances by the **same registry tier caps** the JSON API uses (web/offline ↔ API parity), with the Service-Mode presence token still unlocking (entity-grant **OR** tier-grant). #1358: a new `includes/audio_signing.php` (HMAC signed/expiring URLs, triple-dormant), a gated `audio-media.php` streaming route (Range/206, path-traversal-hardened, `hash_equals`), and a `.htaccess` `/audio/` rewrite — the raw `/data/audio` seal ships **commented** (uncomment per-env, last). Plus the `audio_signing_enabled='0'` migration (rule #19/#20), a Global-Admin **byte-identical no-op verification harness** (`/manage/gating-noop-verify`), and a CI no-op test. No schema beyond the one settings row; no new tier columns (reuses `CanPlayAudio`/`CanDownloadPdf`). Adversarially verified (3 reviewers, 0 blockers). **Operator opt-in required to activate** (provision `AUDIO_SIGNING_KEY` in `.auth/`, run the migration, staged rollout). chore(version): bump to **0.1302.0**.

- fix(live-follow): **"Go Live" code reported as wrong on the follower's device** (#1375). The host (an admin) bypasses maintenance, so "Go Live" mints a code — but the anonymous follower hit a **503 maintenance** response that was shown as "Session not found", blaming a good code. Now the follower (and Service-Mode congregant) gets a distinct "iHymns is briefly down/​unavailable — the code is fine" message and the poll loop keeps following so it resumes. **Codes are also forgiving** now — client + server strip spaces/hyphens/NBSP/smart-punctuation (`"ABC 234"`, `"abc-234"` work), and the entry field disables iOS autocorrect + autocapitalises. (Case was never the cause — it's folded three times.) The join/poll **freshness window widened 90s→180s** plus an immediate heartbeat on tab-visible/focus, so a briefly-backgrounded host session no longer goes stale.
- feat(sotd): **Song of the Day — hemisphere awareness** (#1374). The client sends its hemisphere (derived from its timezone, privacy-safe, no GeoIP), and the **Harvest** theme now falls in each hemisphere's own autumn (Southern = March, Northern = October). Liturgical themes (Advent/Easter/Pentecost…) stay globally synchronous; an old client with no signal is a verified no-op. chore(version): bump to **0.1301.0**.

- chore(version): milestone bump to **0.1300.0** (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81). README + OpenAPI spec updated to match.

- feat(credit-people): **expand Other Identifiers to the authority-control databases + paste-a-URL auto-extract** (#1367). The "Other identifiers" picker grows from VIAF/Wikidata/ORCID to a central registry — adds **GND, FAST, WorldCat, LoC, IdRef, Trove, LibraryThing, Open Library, CiNii** (+ ISNI display), grouped International/National/Academic/People. One registry (`CREDIT_IDENTIFIER_TYPES` in `credit_people_helpers.php`) now drives the dropdown, the save allow-list + per-type value validation, and the public chip link-out — adding a provider is one line, no schema change (`IdentifierType` is already VARCHAR, rule #20). **Auto-extract:** paste a recognised authority URL (e.g. `d-nb.info/gnd/…`, `id.worldcat.org/fast/…`, `entities.oclc.org/worldcat/entity/…`) into an External Link and the matching Other Identifier is detected, the bare ID extracted, and the row auto-added — making rich author data quicker to enter. chore(version): bump to **0.1255.0**.

- fix(places): **live place typeahead always returned HTTP 500** — `manage/places-api.php` required `dirname(__DIR__)/includes/auth.php` (the public path), but the auth helpers live in `manage/includes/auth.php`; the public file doesn't exist, so a `require_once` fatal fired **at load, before the try/catch**, surfacing as a bare 500 with no body (the admin `detail` never rendered). Broken since the endpoint shipped (#996) — curators only ever saw "saved as typed." Now requires `__DIR__/includes/auth.php`; db_mysql.php/places.php stay on the public path (they really are there). Added a `register_shutdown_function` diagnostic so any future load-time fatal returns a visible JSON 500 instead of a silent one (#1365). chore(version): bump to **0.1254.0**.

- chore(version): bump app version to **0.1253.0** (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81). README + OpenAPI spec updated to match.
- feat(credit-people): **partial birth/death dates** — historical writers often have only a known YEAR (or month + year). The editor's date fields now accept **`YYYY`**, **`MM/YYYY`**, or **`DD/MM/YYYY`** (was a strict full-date picker that rejected anything incomplete). Stored as the existing `DATE` column (normalised to the first of the period) plus a new `tblCreditPeople.BirthDatePrecision` / `DeathDatePrecision` (`year|month|day`) so display + round-trip know how much is real (`1823` / `March 1823` / `15 March 1823`). Shared `includes/partial_date.php` parser/formatter; all three save paths (manage add/update + the API) parse + store precision (existence-gated — degrades to a full-date save on an un-migrated env). Run the **Credit-people partial dates** migration card.
- fix(editor): **new songs default to the "Misc" songbook** so auto-save works immediately. A new draft with no songbook silently failed to save (the songbook is mandatory) and curators lost edits believing auto-save had run. New drafts now pre-select Misc (the server already coerced blank → Misc on save), and the empty-songbook check is a **non-blocking warning** rather than a hard error — the save always proceeds (filed under Misc), and changing the songbook reassigns it. No more silent data loss.
- fix(api): **Service Mode "Go Live" fatal — `Call to undefined function checkRateLimit()`** (#1360). `includes/rate_limit.php` (which defines `checkRateLimit()`) was `require_once`'d only inside the `setlist_collab_invite` case, so every other caller (`service_session_start` / `service_broadcast` / `service_operator` / `live_follow_*`) fatal'd. Now loaded top-level in `api.php` so it is always defined. Pre-existing/latent since Service Mode #1335; surfaced on first live use.
- chore(version): bump app version to **0.1250.0** (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81). README + OpenAPI spec updated to match.
- docs(readme): remove the static **Song Library** catalogue block — song counts/songbooks now live in the backend MySQL database (`tblSongbooks` / `tblSongs`), not tied to builds, releases or deployment, so a hard-coded catalogue snapshot in the README only goes stale. Query `tblSongbooks` for the live count.
- feat(api): **server-side content gating + extensible tier registry + read rate limiting + robust CSRF** (#1352 / #1353 / #1354) — turns the previously-advisory gating model into real server-side enforcement, makes the capability set extensible in one line, caps the heaviest public reads, and ends the sporadic "CSRF error" on admin writes. Five strands:
  - **Robust same-origin CSRF** — new `validateCsrfRequest()` in `manage/includes/auth.php` passes a write when EITHER a valid session token is present OR the request is provably same-origin: it requires the `X-Requested-With` header (a browser cannot set it cross-origin without a CORS preflight) AND any present `Origin`/`Referer` host must match `HTTP_HOST`. Because it does not depend on a token that can go stale in a long-lived tab, the intermittent CSRF failures on **duplicate-songs merge/delete**, **places-api** and the editor are fixed. Wired into duplicate-songs merge/delete, places-api, and a single top-level POST gate covering **all** legacy `/manage/editor/api.php` writes (clients already send `X-Requested-With`).
  - **`save_song` moved to the v2 editor API** — the whole-song save now lives in the shared core `manage/editor/save_song_core.php` (`editorSaveSongCore()`), served by **both** `api.php` (a back-compat shim) and `api2.php`; the editor POSTs the save to api2 under its `X-Requested-With` CSRF gate. One save path, no fork.
  - **Extensible tier-capability registry** (#1352) — `TIER_CAPS` in `includes/access_tier_validation.php` is now the single registry for `tblAccessTiers` caps. The 7 original caps stay as their own `TINYINT` columns (the native-app API contract reads them); **new** caps are `'json'`-backed keys inside the additive `tblAccessTiers.Capabilities` column (`migrate-add-tier-capabilities-json.php`, rule #20). **To add a gateable feature, add ONE line to `TIER_CAPS`** (e.g. `'CanRequestSongs' => ['Requests', 'Submit song requests', 'json', 0]`) and run the JSON-backed tier-capabilities migration card on `/manage/setup-database` — the admin checkbox (`manage/tiers.php`), both API CRUD endpoints (`admin_tier_create`/`update`), the `access_tiers` API emit (as `canRequestSongs`) and content gating (`checkTierAccess`) all pick it up with **no schema change**.
  - **Content-gating enforcement** (#1353) — new `includes/content_gating.php::contentGatingApply()` strips gated fields (lyric body, media) from the `song_detail` / `song_data` / `random` API payloads by the requester's tier cap; `ccli_validator.php::checkTierAccess()` now resolves caps from the **live** `tblAccessTiers` row (registry-driven), and the `bulk_audio` offline manifest is entity-gated too. **Entirely dormant and a verified no-op** unless `tblAppSettings.content_gating_enabled='1'`; fail-open and STRICT-safe on un-migrated / edge envs.
  - **Read rate limiting** (#1354) — new `includes/read_rate_limit.php::enforceReadRateLimit()` + `tblReadRateLimit` windowed counter on the heavy public reads (`song_detail` 240/min, `search` 120, `songs_index` 120, `related_songs` 240, bulk 60), keyed **per token where present, else per IP** (a NAT-shared congregation isn't penalised), emitting `429` + `Retry-After`. Fail-open and dormant until `migrate-add-read-rate-limit.php` runs. Window boundaries are computed SQL-side (no per-node clock drift).
  - **XSS hardening** — `JSON_HEX_*` flags added on the remaining inline-script `json_encode` sites so admin-controlled strings can't break out of a `<script>` block.
- feat(gating): **tier-aware gating on the web + offline path** (#1357) — `song.php` (and the `bulk_songs` offline bundle it renders through) now composes the TIER axis with the entity model so it gates copyrighted lyrics consistently with the `song_detail` API. Two independent axes — entity (`require_licence`, authoritative on deny) and tier (the viewer's plan, governs copyrighted) — with a valid Service-Mode presence unlock overriding the tier; so a licence-holder, a premium user and a present congregant are each handled without over-gating. Dormant + fail-open.
- feat(api-platform): **API key usage dashboard + `catalogue:read` keyed reads + dev quickstart + `content:gated` + self-serve requests** — `/manage/api-keys` shows per-key **usage today** + an editable **per-key rate-limit** modal (Phase B); a `catalogue:read` scope lets a trusted integrator read the public catalogue at its **own** per-key limit instead of the anonymous IP limit (Phase A, `enforceReadRateLimitKeyed()`; public stays open); a **developer quickstart** + Swagger Try-it (Phase C). A separate, **dormant `content:gated`** scope lets a deliberately-vetted key read gated/copyrighted content unstripped (a per-partner licensing grant — kept apart from `catalogue:read` so a plain read key never unlocks it). **Self-serve key requests** (Phase D pt 1, new `tblApiKeyRequests` + `request_api_keys` entitlement): an admin requests a `catalogue:read` key with a justification; a global admin reviews the queue and approves (mints + relays) or rejects. Reuses the #1066 key infra. Strategy: `.claude/api-platform-strategy.md` (Phase D webhooks + #1358 audio gating still to come).
- fix(api): **scope + cap the heavy public reads** — `?action=songs` with no `songbook` called `getSongs(null)`, materialising the whole ~3,600-song corpus unauthenticated (the #929 OOM, the same hole `bulk_songs` was hardened against, verified no client used it) — now 400s without a songbook and is rate-limited; `songs_list` rate-limited; `X-RateLimit-Remaining` added.
- fix(csrf): **completed the manage AJAX CSRF sweep** — every AJAX-write admin page (duplicate-songs, places-api, editor, api-keys, tags, languages, activity-log, songbooks) now uses the robust `validateCsrfRequest` (the last three were a client-sends-header/server-ignores-it mismatch); `<form>`-POST pages correctly keep `validateCsrf` (a reload re-bakes the token).
- feat(service-mode): **broadcaster front-ends — projection song-nav + leader device** (#1335) — the two song-driver UIs that let an operator push the congregation's current song to every joined device (the `service_broadcast` endpoint already served both mechanisms). One shared driver core, `js/modules/service-broadcast.js` (`ServiceBroadcaster`): song search over `/api?action=songs_index`, **arrangement-aware** section navigation (projects through the song's `arrangement` so a broadcast lands on the same `.lyric-component` the congregant scrolls to, mirroring `song.php` #160), and the broadcast POST with de-dupe + ended-session teardown — consumed by **both** front-ends (no per-page fork; rule #1/#26). `/manage/service-projection` gains a dockable operator console over the projection overlay (collapsible for a clean code-only screen); new `/manage/service-lead` is the separate leader-device page (lists the operator's active **channel-scoped** sessions → connect-and-drive, a join-code read-out, a 45s keepalive heartbeat, stop-driving / end-service). Adversarially reviewed; two high findings fixed in the same change — a stored-XSS where org-admin-controlled venue/schedule names entered the inline `<script>` without `JSON_HEX_*` (now hardened, the `tiers.php`/`credit-people.php` convention), and the arrangement-blind section index above. Service Mode stays **dormant** (`content_gating_enabled='0'` + no `require_licence:ccli` rows). Remaining rule #26 items — the projection **QR** + a **live multi-device verify** on alpha — tracked in #1339.
- chore(version): bump app version to **0.990.0** (`includes/infoAppVer.php`; the PWA service-worker cache version auto-syncs off it, #81).
- fix(admin): **`/manage/duplicate-songs` rendered a fully blank page** (#1228) — the GET detection path relied on `$db->query()` returning `false` on error, but the DB layer runs mysqli under `MYSQLI_REPORT_STRICT` (it **throws**), so a query against a table unmigrated on the environment (the newer `tblSongLinkSuggestionsDismissed`) white-screened the whole page. Each optional edge-source table is now existence-probed, the `NOT EXISTS(dismissed)` pre-filter is dropped when that table is absent, and the whole detection pass is wrapped in a try/catch that degrades to an actionable error card (pointing at Setup Database) — never a blank page.
- feat(songbooks): **badge unofficial songbooks** (#1223) — the public **/songbooks** list, the **home** songbook grid and the **songbook header** now show a themed **"Unofficial"** badge beside the abbreviation on any book with `tblSongbooks.IsOfficial = 0` (curated groupings / pseudo-songbooks, #502). Official + unofficial books surface together as one "Songbooks" family — **presentation only, no storage merge** (a song's home is `tblSongs.SongbookAbbr NOT NULL`; converting an unofficial book to a Collection would orphan its songs). One shared `.songbook-unofficial-badge` class (Bootstrap 5.3 theme-aware `warning-subtle` tokens → adapts to light / dark / high-contrast); the label is folded into each tile's accessible name (mirrors the language-badge a11y pattern #680/#856) and the word "Unofficial" — not colour alone — carries the meaning (WCAG 1.4.1). Completes the last open item on #1223.
- refactor(admin): **rename "Catalogue" → "Collection"** in the user-facing admin UI (#1223) — page title, headings, buttons, empty-state + member copy, confirm dialogs and every flash message on `/manage/catalogues`, plus the admin-nav entry. The table (`tblCatalogues`/`tblCatalogueSongs`), route (`/manage/catalogues`), activity-log keys (`admin.catalogues.*`), entity type `'catalogue'`, form fields and the `manage_songbooks` entitlement all stay `catalogue` **internally** — owner decision, no data/route migration. Vocabulary folds into #945.
- feat(tags): **canonicalisation suggestions** (#1222) — `/manage/tags` now lists curator-added tags that fuzzily match a standard CCLI/OpenLyrics theme (shared `includes/song_similarity.php` scorer) and offers a one-click **fold-into-standard** that reuses the existing irreversible **Merge** (variant = source/deleted, standard theme = target). Steers ad-hoc free-text tags back toward the canonical vocabulary.
- feat(tags): **standard CCLI / OpenLyrics theme vocabulary** (#1152) — new `appWeb/.sql/migrate-seed-theme-vocabulary.php` adopts the OpenLyrics `themelist.txt` taxonomy (the same vocabulary as CCLI SongSelect) so Browse-by-Theme is interoperable with the wider worship-software ecosystem. Strictly-additive, idempotent migration adds `tblSongTags.ParentId` (self-FK → 2-level Parent/Child hierarchy), `tblSongTags.CcliThemeId` (dormant SongSelect theme number) and `tblSongTags.Source` (`'curator'` | `'ccli-openlyrics'`); leaf names live in `Name`, the path via `ParentId`; same-named curator tags are promoted to the standard source rather than duplicated. Surfaced with its hierarchy on `/manage/tags`.
- feat(admin): **Duplicate & Counterpart Review** (#1215) — `/manage/duplicate-songs` reworked from a flat same-title / ISRC / URL **merge** list into one scored, sectioned review surface that absorbs the old `/manage/song-link-suggestions` page (#807/#808). Every candidate group now shows a **probability %** + the signals that fired (title · first lyric line · shared writer · shared **ISWC / CCLI / ISRC**), via a new shared scorer `includes/song_similarity.php` that also lights up the dormant `tblSongLinkSuggestions.Confidence` / `Signal` columns from #1066 (#1216). Candidates are clustered by union-find over exact-title, hard-identifier, shared-URL and fuzzy-suggestion edges, so a retitled arrangement that shares an ISWC now groups with its original. Three sections by book span: **Cross-book counterparts** (one-click **Link as counterparts** — bulk, writes `tblSongLinks`; #1219), **Same title in one official songbook** (almost always *different hymns* — merge guarded behind a type-to-confirm + server `force` flag; #1218), and **Same title in one collection** (probable import duplicate). Adds per-group **Dismiss** + **Rebuild**. The page is now curator-visible (`edit_songs`) for Link/Dismiss while the destructive **Merge** stays admin-gated (`manage_duplicate_songs`); `/manage/song-link-suggestions` 302-redirects to the unified page and the nav entry is relabelled "Duplicates & Links" (#1220). Deferred signal ideas tracked in #1221.
- feat(catalogue): **Works — composition grouping** (#840) — new top-level `tblWorks` entity that groups multiple `tblSongs` rows representing the same underlying composition across different songbooks / arrangements / translations. Mirrors the MusicBrainz Work ↔ Recording relationship. Optional ISWC, optional notes, unlimited parent / child nesting (cycle-prevention enforced server-side at update time). New `/work/<slug>` public page with categorised "Find this work elsewhere" panel, and a "Part of work" + sibling-version list on every song page that belongs to one. New `/manage/works` admin CRUD page gated on the `manage_works` entitlement.
- feat(admin): **URL → provider auto-detect** (#841) for the External-Links card-list editor. Paste a Wikipedia / YouTube / Spotify / etc. URL and the provider dropdown auto-selects the matching registry entry. Single global module (`js/modules/external-link-detect.js`) loaded on every `/manage/*` page; one-line `attachAutoDetect(rowEl)` wiring per consumer. 30+ providers covered, full unit-test coverage in `tests/test-external-link-detect.js`.
- refactor(admin): **URL-detect rules in MySQL** (#845) — replaces the JS-hard-coded `RULES` array with a curator-editable `tblExternalLinkPatterns` table. Each link type can carry many host / path patterns; sub-domain matching (suffix vs exact) and optional path-prefix discrimination are both supported. New `/manage/external-link-types` admin page (gated on `manage_external_link_types`). Pages that ship `_iHymnsLinkTypes` to JS attach each type's patterns via the shared `attachExternalLinkPatterns()` helper. JS falls back to the bundled `RULES` array on pre-migration deployments — no behaviour change at first install.
- refactor(admin): **responsive list-view convention** (#842) — `.admin-table-responsive` + `data-col-priority="primary|secondary|tertiary"` lets pages opt into a column-priority hide-on-narrow rule. Below 768 px secondary + tertiary columns hide; below 992 px tertiary only. Pages opted in this PR: Credit People (the original phone-clipping motivator), Songbooks, Songbook Series, Works. Convention documented in `DEV_NOTES.md`.
- feat(admin): **bulk-promote in-use Credit People into the register** (#846) — new `/manage/credit-people-bulk-promote` page lists every distinct name cited on a song-credit row that doesn't have a `tblCreditPeople` row yet, scores each against existing registry rows + sibling candidates with a Levenshtein + token-set blend, and lets the curator pick per-row Register / Merge-into-existing / Skip. The whole submit runs in one transaction with a shared `bulk_run_id` on the audit log so the run is reviewable as a unit. CTA on `/manage/credit-people` only renders when there's unregistered-but-cited work waiting.
- docs: **comprehensive refresh** (#843) — visitor in-app help adds an "External links & Works" section; admin in-app help adds Works, External Links and Mobile Admin sections; OpenAPI spec adds `Work` schema + `ExternalLink` shared schema + `/api?action=work&slug=…` endpoint; `DEV_NOTES.md` documents the external-links data shape, Works schema and the responsive convention.

- feat(songs): **cross-book counterparts** (#807) — new `tblSongLinks` join table + editor sidebar section + public song-page "Also appears in" dropdown + `api.php?action=song_links` endpoint. Lets curators declare that two-or-more `tblSongs` rows in different songbooks are the same hymn (e.g. Amazing Grace as MP-031 / CH-376 / SDAH-108 / SoF-29 / JP-006). Distinct from `tblSongTranslations` (different language) and `tblSongbooks.ParentSongbookId` (translated / edition derivatives at songbook level).
- feat(admin): **song link suggestions** (#808) — new `/manage/song-link-suggestions` page lists pre-computed similar-titled-song candidates with Link / Dismiss / Open-in-editor actions. Builder script at `tools/build-song-link-suggestions.php` computes pairwise title + first-line + author similarity, writing into `tblSongLinkSuggestions`. Inline 5-suggestion panel in the editor sidebar surfaces candidates for the open song.

## [0.10.x] — 2026-04-18 (alpha)

- feat: **auth** — magic-link (email + 6-digit code) is now the primary sign-in path; cross-subdomain `HttpOnly` cookie + 30-day sliding expiry; HttpOnly cookie ITP-resistant persistence
- feat: **admin portal** — `/manage/` dashboard now shows Library + People & Activity stat groups, recent-users panel, quick-link tiles for every admin surface; `/admin/` alias redirects to `/manage/`
- feat: **channel gating** — alpha/beta subdomains gated by `access_alpha` / `access_beta` entitlements; embedded magic-link form on the gate page
- feat: **entitlements system** — granular role→capability map with a `/manage/entitlements` editor; overrides stored in `tblAppSettings`; client-side mirror for UI decisions
- feat: **analytics** — `/manage/analytics` dashboard (7/30/90-day range) with Top Songs, Top Songbooks, Top Search Queries, Zero-Result Queries; per-panel CSV export
- feat: **song requests** — public `/request-a-song` form with rate limit + honeypot; admin triage queue at `/manage/requests`
- feat: **editor per-song save** — `/api?action=save_song` UPSERT endpoint; auto-save now per-song (not full-corpus)
- feat: **editor multi-select** — bulk delete with typed-DELETE safety for ≥10 rows
- feat: **song revision audit log** — `tblSongRevisions` writes on every per-song save
- feat: **scripture search** — abbreviation expansion (`Ps 23` → `Psalm 23`) + tag-based curated match merged to top of results
- feat: **practice mode** — Full / Dimmed / Hidden cycle for memorisation; tap-to-reveal
- feat: **keyboard shortcuts toggle** — Settings → Accessibility lets users turn shortcuts on/off
- feat: **PWA update toast** — "New version — Refresh" reliably appears when SW deploys
- feat: **PWA offline audio** — on-demand caching on playback; `/api?action=bulk_audio` for pre-caching; Settings toggle
- feat: **setlist scheduling API** — `setlist_schedule_set / _clear / _upcoming` endpoints
- feat: **backup restore** — admin UI with server-list and upload-from-device paths; audit log on upload
- feat: **Misc songbook** — songs now permitted without a song number (schema `Number` is NULL-able); book-glyph badge renders instead of `#null`
- feat: **Title Case** rendering everywhere song titles are displayed
- feat: **admin area re-skin** — unified with main-site palette (indigo/violet accent) via shared `css/admin.css`
- fix: **Settings Account card** — now flips live on auth-state change via `ihymns:auth-changed` event bus
- fix: **editor Save button** — renamed from "Save JSON" with clearer DB-first wording
- fix: **Usage Stats badges** — consistent width regardless of digit count
- security: CSRF tokens on all admin POST forms; DB error messages no longer leaked to clients

# 📝 iHymns — Changelog

> All notable changes to this project are documented here.
> Format follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).
> Versioning follows [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).
> `v1.x.x` = Phase 1 (local JSON) | `v2.x.x` = Phase 2 (iLyrics dB)

---

## [Unreleased]

### 📋 Added — 2026-04-05

- Created `Project_Plan.md` — comprehensive project plan with technology stack, architecture, milestones
- Created `PROJECT_STATUS.md` — project status tracker
- Created `CHANGELOG.md` — this changelog
- Created `DEV_NOTES.md` — developer notes
- Created `README.md` — project overview with structure diagram
- Created `.claude/ProjectBrief.md` — Claude AI context and project brief
- Created `.gitignore` — comprehensive ignore rules for macOS, Windows, Linux, VS Code, Xcode, Node.js, Android
- Created `help/` documentation — getting started, searching, songbooks, favorites, troubleshooting, FAQ
- Analysed existing song data: 5 songbooks, ~7,415 source files
- Documented song data format and structure
- Created `package.json` — Node.js project configuration (v1.0.0-alpha.1)
- Built `tools/parse-songs.js` — song data parser (fully annotated, ~500 lines)
- Generated `data/songs.json` — 3,612 songs parsed (5.22 MB), with structured components, writer/composer credits, audio/sheet music flags
- 31 songs have no lyrics (empty source files — placeholder entries in source data)
- Built Web PWA core (Milestone 2) in `appWeb/public_html/`:
  - `index.php` — main SPA layout with Bootstrap 5.3, responsive navbar, search, dark mode toggle
  - `includes/infoAppVer.php` — centralised version metadata with auto-computed copyright year
  - `css/styles.css` — custom styles: songbook cards, song list, lyrics view, dark mode, animations
  - `css/print.css` — print-optimised stylesheet for song lyrics
  - `js/app.js` — main entry point: data loading, SPA router, module orchestration
  - `js/utils/helpers.js` — shared DOM helpers, text formatting, hash routing, debounce
  - `js/modules/songbook.js` — songbook grid (home) and song list views
  - `js/modules/song-view.js` — song detail view with formatted lyrics, breadcrumb, actions
  - `js/modules/search.js` — Fuse.js fuzzy search (title, lyrics, songbook, writer, number)
  - `js/modules/favorites.js` — localStorage-based favourites management
  - `js/modules/settings.js` — dark mode, PWA install banner, update checker
  - `manifest.json` — PWA manifest for installability
  - `service-worker.js` — offline caching (cache-first for static, network-first for data)
  - `assets/favicon.svg` — SVG favicon (music notes on purple circle)

### 🏗 Changed — 2026-04-05

- Set up new project directory structure: `appWeb/`, `apple/`, `android/`, `tools/`, `data/`, `help/`, `.github/workflows/`
- Configured `appWeb/` with deployment structure: `public_html/`, `data_share/`, `private_html/`
- Added song editor feature to Phase 1 plan (developer tool in `appWeb/private_html/editor/`)
- Added smart install banner feature (PWA install or app store links)
- Added auto-update checking with regular polling
- Added Apple TV remote control feature to Phase 2 plan
- Added automated semver versioning (`v1.x.x` Phase 1, `v2.x.x` Phase 2)
- Added SFTP deployment pipeline design (modelled on phpWhoIs)

### 🗑 Removed — 2026-04-05

- Deleted `appFlutter/` — old Flutter implementation (replaced by native apps)
- Deleted `appAppleIOS/` — old Swift implementation (starting fresh with Swift 6.3/SwiftUI)
- Deleted `backend/` — old PHP API + SQL (replaced by JSON data layer in Phase 1)
- Deleted `notes` — old ChatGPT reference link

### 🎫 GitHub Issues — 2026-04-05

- Created 49 Phase 1 issues (#1–#63, odd numbers + extras)
- Created 14 Phase 2 issues (#2–#30, even numbers)
- Created 7 infrastructure issues (#64–#70) for SFTP deployment, semver, CI/CD
- Created custom labels: `phase-1`, `phase-2`, `platform-web`, `platform-apple`, `platform-android`, `infrastructure`, `data`, `tooling`
